/**
 * Rewrites every frontmatter array key and body link that points at `targetSlug`
 * to `nextSlug`. The core operation behind rename_concept and merge_concepts.
 *
 * Matching policy (same as findBacklinks):
 *  - absolute slug (`capabilities/mcp-server`)
 *  - last segment (`mcp-server`) — the replacement uses `nextSlug`'s tail rather
 *    than keeping the target tail, so a rename shows the new name consistently
 *    whichever form the slug was written in
 *  - trailing match (`…/mcp-server`) follows the same policy
 *
 * Body replacement covers `[[targetSlug]]` and `(targetSlug.md)`.
 *
 * `options.dryRun = true` previews without writing to disk.
 * `options.excludeSlugs` lets the caller skip documents it replaces in the same plan.
 *
 * Returns `{ updates: [{ slug, beforeKeys, afterKeys, bodyHit }], totalUpdated }`.
 */
export function redirectBacklinks(rootPath, targetSlug, nextSlug, options = {}) {
  /**
   * With `deferWrite: true` only the plan is built and returned as `plan`; disk is
   * untouched. Different from `dryRun`: a dry run is a preview *for the user*,
   * while this lets **the caller merge its own writes into one plan** and apply
   * them all-or-nothing. `rename_concept` uses it to bind file creation, backlink
   * rewriting, and old-file deletion into one unit.
   */
  const { dryRun = false, deferWrite = false, excludeSlugs = [] } = options;
  const excluded = new Set(Array.isArray(excludeSlugs) ? excludeSlugs : []);
  if (typeof targetSlug !== 'string' || !targetSlug) {
    throw new Error('targetSlug is required.');
  }
  if (typeof nextSlug !== 'string' || !nextSlug) {
    throw new Error('nextSlug is required.');
  }
  if (targetSlug === nextSlug) {
    return { updates: [], totalUpdated: 0, plan: [] };
  }

  const docs = loadVaultDocs(rootPath);
  const targetTail = targetSlug.split('/').pop();
  const nextTail = nextSlug.split('/').pop();
  const tailMatches = docs
    .map((doc) => doc.slug)
    .filter((slug) => slug.split('/').pop() === targetTail);
  // A bare/suffix tail is shorthand only while it uniquely resolves. When
  // capabilities/foo and elements/foo both exist, rewriting "foo" would
  // silently redirect whichever concept the author meant and can even mutate
  // the other node's frontmatter slug. Exact canonical refs remain safe.
  const canRewriteTail = tailMatches.length === 1 && tailMatches[0] === targetSlug;

  function rewriteArrayItem(value) {
    if (typeof value !== 'string') return { value, changed: false };
    if (value === targetSlug) return { value: nextSlug, changed: true };
    if (canRewriteTail && value === targetTail) return { value: nextTail, changed: true };
    if (canRewriteTail && value.endsWith(`/${targetTail}`)) {
      // path-prefixed tail — preserved prefix plus the new tail
      const prefix = value.slice(0, value.length - targetTail.length);
      return { value: `${prefix}${nextTail}`, changed: true };
    }
    return { value, changed: false };
  }

  const updates = [];
  /** The write plan destined for disk: applied in one go once the loop ends. */
  const plan = [];
  for (const doc of docs) {
    if (doc.slug === targetSlug || excluded.has(doc.slug)) continue;
    const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, doc.slug);
    const nextFm = { ...doc.frontmatter };
    const beforeKeys = [];
    const afterKeys = [];
    let fmChanged = false;

    for (const key of Object.keys(nextFm)) {
      const value = nextFm[key];
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const before = [...value];
        const after = value.map((v) => rewriteArrayItem(v).value);
        if (before.some((b, i) => b !== after[i])) {
          // dedup + sort — never append a duplicate when nextSlug is already
          // present, so the same graph state leaves the same frontmatter array.
          const deduped = normalizeRelationRefs(after);
          nextFm[key] = deduped;
          beforeKeys.push({ key, before });
          afterKeys.push({ key, after: deduped });
          fmChanged = true;
        }
      } else if (typeof value === 'string') {
        // Only graph reference slots are rewritten (`domain:` plus the
        // GRAPH_ARRAY_KEYS family). An evidence string such as `path:` is not a
        // reference — measured 2026-08-01 while flattening the dogfood vault: the
        // tail-suffix clause of the rename `elements/src/widgets/docs-vault` →
        // `elements/docs-vault-widget` also rewrote **another node's**
        // `path: src/entities/docs-vault` to `…/docs-vault-widget`, pointing it at
        // a file that does not exist (3 cases of pathDrift).
        const isRefSlot = key === 'domain' || GRAPH_ARRAY_KEY_SET.has(key);
        const r = isRefSlot ? rewriteArrayItem(value) : { changed: false };
        if (r.changed) {
          nextFm[key] = r.value;
          beforeKeys.push({ key, before: value });
          afterKeys.push({ key, after: r.value });
          fmChanged = true;
        }
      } else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
        // The KEY of an object map value (`relation_notes: {ref: "why"}`) is a
        // rename target too. Without this, a relation's rationale note is orphaned
        // the moment the rename happens — the red-team finding that blocked the
        // schema from shipping.
        //
        // Key-collision merge policy: when both the old and the new key exist, the
        // existing (new) value wins — a note the user already wrote under the new
        // name is the more recent intent, and letting the rename overwrite it is
        // silent data loss. The displaced old value is not discarded: it stays in
        // beforeKeys, visible in the dry run and the audit.
        const entries = Object.entries(value);
        let mapChanged = false;
        const nextMap = {};
        for (const [mapKey, mapValue] of entries) {
          const r = rewriteArrayItem(mapKey);
          if (!r.changed) {
            if (!(mapKey in nextMap)) nextMap[mapKey] = mapValue;
            continue;
          }
          mapChanged = true;
          if (r.value in nextMap || entries.some(([k]) => k === r.value)) {
            // Collision — the existing (new key) value wins; the old value is only recorded.
            continue;
          }
          nextMap[r.value] = mapValue;
        }
        if (mapChanged) {
          // Preserve the collision winner (the original new key) value
          for (const [mapKey, mapValue] of entries) {
            if (!(mapKey in nextMap) && !rewriteArrayItem(mapKey).changed) nextMap[mapKey] = mapValue;
          }
          beforeKeys.push({ key, before: value });
          afterKeys.push({ key, after: nextMap });
          nextFm[key] = nextMap;
          fmChanged = true;
        }
      }
    }

    let nextBody = doc.body;
    let bodyChanged = false;
    if (nextBody.includes(`[[${targetSlug}]]`)) {
      nextBody = nextBody.split(`[[${targetSlug}]]`).join(`[[${nextSlug}]]`);
      bodyChanged = true;
    }
    if (canRewriteTail && nextBody.includes(`[[${targetTail}]]`)) {
      nextBody = nextBody.split(`[[${targetTail}]]`).join(`[[${nextTail}]]`);
      bodyChanged = true;
    }
    if (nextBody.includes(`(${targetSlug}.md)`)) {
      nextBody = nextBody.split(`(${targetSlug}.md)`).join(`(${nextSlug}.md)`);
      bodyChanged = true;
    }

    if (!fmChanged && !bodyChanged) continue;

    updates.push({
      slug: doc.slug,
      title: docTitle(doc),
      beforeKeys,
      afterKeys,
      bodyChanged,
    });

    // **Do not write here.** This line used to be a `writeFileSync`, so a failure
    // on the next file left a half vault with only the earlier ones changed.
    plan.push({
      op: 'write',
      path: filePath,
      content: buildMarkdown({ frontmatter: nextFm, body: nextBody }),
      /*
       * **Carry the read timestamp along** (review 2026-08-16).
       *
       * This document is a snapshot read seconds or minutes ago. If the user
       * edited the file in their own editor in between, writing it as-is here
       * destroys that edit. Compare this value against disk immediately before
       * the write.
       */
      expectedMtime: doc.mtime,
      expectedRaw: doc.raw,
    });
  }

  if (!dryRun && !deferWrite) applyAllOrNothing(plan, { requireRevisions: true });

  return {
    updates,
    totalUpdated: updates.length,
    ...(deferWrite ? { plan } : {}),
  };
}

function docTitle(doc) {
  if (typeof doc?.frontmatter?.title === 'string' && doc.frontmatter.title.trim()) {
    return doc.frontmatter.title;
  }
  if (typeof doc?.frontmatter?.name === 'string' && doc.frontmatter.name.trim()) {
    return doc.frontmatter.name;
  }
  return doc?.slug;
  return doc?.slug;
}

function normalizeForDuplicateTitle(title) {
  return String(title ?? '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

/**
 * Returns an advisory warning string when a new node's title matches an existing
 * node's under normalisation (lowercased, whitespace collapsed), else null.
 *
 * The #1 failure mode of a growing vault is **duplicate or hallucinated nodes**.
 * Checking `similar_nodes` before `add_concept` is the correct discipline, but
 * forgetting it lets near-duplicates pile up quietly. This is add_concept's safety
 * net: when the same title already exists, it says "merge with patch_concept". It
 * is advisory and never blocks the write.
 *
 * Precision first — *exact* match after normalisation — to minimise false alarms.
 * Fuzzy or partial matching is excluded because it fires on genuinely different
 * concepts (auth-login vs auth-logout). The node itself (same slug) and empty
 * titles are excluded.
 */
export function detectDuplicateTitle(title, slug, docs) {
  const norm = normalizeForDuplicateTitle(title);
  if (!norm) return null;
  for (const doc of docs ?? []) {
    if (!doc || doc.slug === slug) continue;
    if (normalizeForDuplicateTitle(docTitle(doc)) === norm) {
      const kind = doc.frontmatter?.kind ?? 'unknown';
      return (
        `a node titled "${title}" already exists at "${doc.slug}" (kind: ${kind}): ` +
        `if this is the same concept, patch_concept on "${doc.slug}" instead of adding a duplicate.`
      );
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Light check that the vault root looks like a markdown vault. Only an absolute
 * path and a directory are required — a folder with no frontmatter is allowed as

export function ensureVaultRoot(rootPath) {
  if (!rootPath) {
    throw new Error('Set the vault root via OATLAS_VAULT env var or --vault arg.');
  }
  if (!existsSync(rootPath)) {
    throw new Error(`Vault root not found: ${rootPath}`);
  }
  if (!statSync(rootPath).isDirectory()) {
    throw new Error(`Vault root is not a directory: ${rootPath}`);
  }

function attachVaultValidation(result, args = {}) {
  const validation = validateVaultTool({});
  const pathsChecked = validation.pathDrift?.checked !== false;
  const driftCount = validation.pathDrift?.drifts?.length ?? 0;
  const errorCount = validation.summary.errorFiles;
  const frontmatterWarnings = validation.summary.warningFiles;
  const warningCount = frontmatterWarnings + driftCount;
  // **The sentence states what this check looked at.** It used to merge two kinds
  // of warning into one number and say only "validator or source-path warning(s)".
  // So when `health` reported warn:13 on a vault that `validate` called clean, the
  // user had no way to tell whether those 13 were frontmatter or code paths.
  const scopeTail = pathsChecked
    ? ` (frontmatter/graph refs ${frontmatterWarnings}, source paths ${driftCount}; repoRoot ${validation.pathDrift?.repoRoot ?? 'unknown'})`
    : ' (frontmatter/graph refs only — source paths were NOT checked; pass repoRoot / OATLAS_REPO_ROOT to include them)';

export function finalizeProjectMeaningReceipt({
  vaultRoot,
  projectSlug,
  projectBody,
  graphHash,
  sourceFingerprint,
  measuredAt,
}) {
  if (typeof vaultRoot !== 'string' || vaultRoot.length === 0) throw new Error('vaultRoot is required');
  if (!safeProjectSlug(projectSlug)) throw new Error('projectSlug is invalid');
  if (!safeGraphHash(graphHash)) throw new Error('graphHash is invalid');
  if (!safeOpaque(sourceFingerprint)) throw new Error('sourceFingerprint is invalid');
  if (!safeMeasuredAt(measuredAt)) throw new Error('measuredAt is invalid');
  parseProjectCompetencyMarkdown(projectBody);

  const existing = readState(vaultRoot);
  if (existing.status === 'unsafe_path') throw existing.error;
  if (existing.status === 'malformed') throw new Error('Existing project meaning receipt is malformed');
  const receipt = {
    version: PROJECT_MEANING_RECEIPT_VERSION,
    evaluator: MEANING_COMPETENCY_EVALUATOR,
    markdownContract: PROJECT_COMPETENCY_MARKDOWN_CONTRACT,
    projectSlug,
    bodyDigest: bodyDigest(projectBody),
    graphHash,
    sourceFingerprint,
    measuredAt,
  };
  const retained = (existing.state?.receipts ?? []).filter((row) => row.projectSlug !== projectSlug);
  const state = {
    version: PROJECT_MEANING_RECEIPT_VERSION,
    receipts: [...retained, receipt].sort((left, right) => left.projectSlug.localeCompare(right.projectSlug)),
  };
  replaceVaultSidecarText(
    vaultRoot,
    PROJECT_MEANING_STATE_FILENAME,
    `${JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)}\n`,
    { expectedRevision: existing.revision },
  );
  return receipt;
}

      evaluator: MEANING_COMPETENCY_EVALUATOR,
      graphHash: input.graphHash,
      inventory: input.inventory,
      questions: [],
    },
  });

    46	 * External-change detection — blocks a silent overwrite when a human GUI, an
    47	 * outside editor, or another AI's MCP server touches the same `.md` concurrently.
    48	 *
    49	 * When the caller passes `expectedMtime`, the current mtime is compared just
    50	 * before the write and a conflict throws, leaving the caller to tell the user and
    51	 * decide whether to force. Omitting the option skips the check, which keeps
    52	 * existing callers working.
    53	 *
    54	 * mtime is an integer in ms. Filesystems differ in precision, but MCP calls are
    55	 * infrequent enough that 1s-granularity detection suffices.
    56	 */
    57	export class VaultConflictError extends Error {
    58	  constructor(slug, expectedMtime, currentMtime) {
    59	    super(
    60	      `Vault conflict: "${slug}" was modified externally (changed on disk) between read and write. ` +
    61	        `expectedMtime=${expectedMtime} currentMtime=${currentMtime}. ` +
    62	        // **Never name a recovery path that does not exist** (measured 2026-07-29).
    63	        //
    64	        // The old wording said to overwrite with `force:true`. Of the eight write
    65	        // tools that raise this error, **seven do not declare `force` at all** —
    66	        // trying it yields `unknown_argument`. The one that does accept it,
    67	        // `delete_concept`, means "delete even with backlinks", not "ignore
    68	        // mtime", so it comes back as `vault_conflict` too.
    69	        //
    70	        // The advertised recovery was therefore dead in all eight tools, and an
    71	        // agent that believes the message fails a second time. State only the
    72	        // path that actually works.
    73	        `Re-read the doc with get_concept to get the current expected_mtime, then retry the write.`,
    74	    );
    75	    this.name = 'VaultConflictError';
    76	    this.code = 'VAULT_CONFLICT';
    77	    this.slug = slug;
    78	    this.expectedMtime = expectedMtime;
    79	    this.currentMtime = currentMtime;
    80	  }
    81	}

    83	/**
    84	 * File mtime (ms), or null when the file is absent. In a read-modify-write flow
    85	 * the caller captures it right after the read and passes it as `expectedMtime`.
    86	 */
    87	export function getFileMtime(filePath) {
    88	  try {
    89	    return statSync(filePath).mtimeMs;
    90	  } catch {
    91	    return null;
    92	  }
    93	}

    95	function sameFileSnapshot(left, right) {
    96	  return left.dev === right.dev
    97	    && left.ino === right.ino
    98	    && left.size === right.size
    99	    && left.mtimeMs === right.mtimeMs
   100	    && left.ctimeMs === right.ctimeMs;
   101	}
   102	
   103	/** Reads bytes and metadata together from one open file, and confirms the current path is still that file. */
   104	function readStableFileSnapshot(filePath) {
   105	  let lastError = null;
   106	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
   107	    let descriptor = null;
   108	    try {
   109	      descriptor = openSync(filePath, 'r');
   110	      const before = fstatSync(descriptor);
   111	      const raw = readFileSync(descriptor, 'utf-8');
   112	      const after = fstatSync(descriptor);
   113	      closeSync(descriptor);
   114	      descriptor = null;
   115	      const currentPath = statSync(filePath);
   116	      if (sameFileSnapshot(before, after) && sameFileSnapshot(after, currentPath)) {
   117	        return { raw, mtime: after.mtimeMs };
   118	      }
   119	    } catch (error) {
   120	      lastError = error;
   121	    } finally {
   122	      if (descriptor !== null) {
   123	        try {
   124	          closeSync(descriptor);
   125	        } catch {
   126	          /* already closed */
   127	        }
   128	      }
   129	    }
   130	  }
   131	  const reason = lastError?.message ? ` (${lastError.message})` : '';
   132	  throw new Error(`Could not capture a stable file snapshot: ${filePath}${reason}`);
   133	}
   134	
   135	function assertSnapshotMtime(slug, expectedMtime, currentMtime) {
   136	  if (expectedMtime === null || expectedMtime === undefined) return;
   137	  if (currentMtime === null || Math.abs(currentMtime - expectedMtime) >= 1) {
   138	    throw new VaultConflictError(slug, expectedMtime, currentMtime);
   139	  }
   140	}
   141	
   142	function assertCurrentDocSnapshot(slug, filePath, expectedRaw, expectedMtime) {
   143	  if (!fileMatchesExpectedRaw(filePath, expectedRaw)) {
   144	    throw new VaultConflictError(slug, expectedMtime, getFileMtime(filePath));
   145	  }
   146	}

   149	  if (value === null || Array.isArray(value) || typeof value !== 'object') {
   150	    throw new Error(`${name} must be an object.`);

   588	 *

   590	 * later write is what makes conflict detection work.
   591	 */
   592	export function readDoc(rootPath, filePath) {
   593	  const snapshot = readStableFileSnapshot(filePath);
   594	  const raw = snapshot.raw;
   595	  const { frontmatter, body, diagnostics } = parseFrontmatter(raw);
   596	  const result = {

   598	    frontmatter,
   599	    body,
   600	    raw,
   601	    mtime: snapshot.mtime,
   602	  };
   603	  if (diagnostics?.length) result.diagnostics = diagnostics;
   604	  return result;
   605	}

  1226	 * Writes a new doc, creating directories as needed. Throws if the file exists —
  1227	 * an overwrite has to be the caller's explicit choice.
  1228	 */
  1229	export function writeDoc(rootPath, slug, { frontmatter, body = '' }) {
  1230	  const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, slug);
  1231	  if (existsSync(filePath)) {
  1232	    throw new Error(
  1233	      `Doc already exists at "${slug}". To update fields, use patch_concept(slug, frontmatter, body, expected_mtime). To rename, use rename_concept(oldSlug, newSlug). Never delete-then-add: that loses backlinks.`,
  1234	    );
  1235	  }
  1236	  assertPlainObject(frontmatter, 'frontmatter');
  1237	  if (typeof body !== 'string') {
  1238	    throw new Error('body must be a string.');
  1239	  }
  1240	  // Slug flatness — measured at the one door where a new identity is born. This
  1241	  // is shape validity, so it is a hard error (the fan-out gate's "never block"
  1242	  // principle covers judgements of meaning only).
  1243	  const slugIssue = flatSlugIssue(frontmatter?.kind, slug);
  1244	  if (slugIssue) throw new Error(slugIssue);
  1245	  assertNodeIdentity(rootPath, slug, frontmatter);
  1246	  mkdirSync(dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  1247	  return commitDoc(rootPath, slug, filePath, frontmatter, body, { created: true });
  1248	}

  1250	/**
  1251	 * Options shape for patchFrontmatter / updateDoc / deleteDoc / redirectBacklinks:
  1252	 *   { expectedMtime?: number }
  1253	 *
  1254	 * When the caller passes the mtime it read, a change detected just before the
  1255	 * write throws a conflict. Omitting it skips the check, keeping existing callers
  1256	 * working.
  1257	 */
  1258	
  1259	/**
  1260	 * Deletes a doc permanently. Confirmation and backlink checks are the caller's
  1261	 * responsibility. Returns `{ slug, filePath, frontmatter, body, raw, mtime }`
  1262	 * captured just before the delete, throws when the file is absent, and honours
  1263	 * `expectedMtime` for external-change detection.
  1264	 */
  1265	export function deleteDoc(rootPath, slug, options = {}) {
  1266	  const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, slug);
  1267	  if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
  1268	    throw new Error(`Doc not found: "${slug}". ${notFoundSuffix(rootPath, slug)}`);
  1269	  }
  1270	  const captured = readDoc(rootPath, filePath);
  1271	  assertSnapshotMtime(slug, options.expectedMtime, captured.mtime);
  1272	  options.beforeDelete?.();
  1273	  assertCurrentDocSnapshot(slug, filePath, captured.raw, captured.mtime);
  1274	  unlinkSync(filePath);
  1275	  return { ...captured, filePath };
  1276	}
  1277	
  1278	/**
  1279	 * Patches only the frontmatter of an existing doc, preserving the body. In the
  1280	 * patch object, `null` deletes the key and `undefined` skips it.
  1281	 * `options.expectedMtime` enables external-change detection.
  1282	 *
  1283	 * Returns `{ filePath, frontmatter, mintedUid }`. `mintedUid` has a value **only
  1284	 * when this write minted the identity for the first time** (recovering a
  1285	 * hand-authored node) — the caller must tell the person, because identity coming
  1286	 * into existence is not an event to pass over silently.
  1287	 */
  1288	export function patchFrontmatter(rootPath, slug, patch, options = {}) {
  1289	  const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, slug);
  1290	  if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
  1291	    throw new Error(`Doc not found: "${slug}". ${notFoundSuffix(rootPath, slug)}`);
  1292	  }
  1293	  assertPlainObject(patch, 'frontmatter');
  1294	  const doc = readDoc(rootPath, filePath);
  1295	  assertSnapshotMtime(slug, options.expectedMtime, doc.mtime);
  1296	  const { frontmatter, body } = doc;
  1297	  assertIdentityPatch(frontmatter, patch);
  1298	  const next = { ...frontmatter };
  1299	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
  1300	    if (value === null) {
  1301	      delete next[key];
  1302	    } else if (value !== undefined) {
  1303	      next[key] = normalizeFrontmatterValue(key, value);
  1304	    }
  1305	  }
  1306	  const mintedUid = fillMissingUid(frontmatter, next);
  1307	  assertNodeIdentity(rootPath, slug, next);
  1308	  commitDoc(rootPath, slug, filePath, next, body, {
  1309	    previousFrontmatter: frontmatter,
  1310	    expectedRaw: doc.raw,
  1311	    expectedMtime: doc.mtime,
  1312	    beforeCommit: options.beforeCommit,
  1313	  });
  1314	  return { filePath, frontmatter: next, mintedUid };
  1315	}
  1316	
  1317	/**
  1318	 * Updates frontmatter and body of an existing doc together. Frontmatter patch
  1319	 * semantics match patchFrontmatter (`null` deletes, `undefined` skips); a string
  1320	 * `body` replaces, `undefined` preserves. `expectedMtime` enables
  1321	 * external-change detection.
  1322	 *
  1323	 * The return contract matches `patchFrontmatter`: `{ filePath, frontmatter, mintedUid }`.
  1324	 */
  1325	export function updateDoc(rootPath, slug, {
  1326	  frontmatter: patch,
  1327	  body,
  1328	  expectedMtime,
  1329	  beforeCommit,
  1330	}) {
  1331	  const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, slug);
  1332	  if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
  1333	    throw new Error(`Doc not found: "${slug}". ${notFoundSuffix(rootPath, slug)}`);
  1334	  }
  1335	  assertOptionalPlainObject(patch, 'frontmatter');
  1336	  const doc = readDoc(rootPath, filePath);
  1337	  assertSnapshotMtime(slug, expectedMtime, doc.mtime);
  1338	  const { frontmatter, body: oldBody } = doc;
  1339	  assertIdentityPatch(frontmatter, patch);
  1340	  const nextFm = { ...frontmatter };
  1341	  if (patch !== undefined) {
  1342	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
  1343	      if (value === null) {
  1344	        delete nextFm[key];
  1345	      } else if (value !== undefined) {
  1346	        nextFm[key] = normalizeFrontmatterValue(key, value);
  1347	      }
  1348	    }
  1349	  }
  1350	  if (body !== undefined && typeof body !== 'string') {
  1351	    throw new Error('body must be a string.');
  1352	  }
  1353	  const nextBody = body === undefined ? oldBody : body;
  1354	  const mintedUid = fillMissingUid(frontmatter, nextFm);
  1355	  assertNodeIdentity(rootPath, slug, nextFm);
  1356	  commitDoc(rootPath, slug, filePath, nextFm, nextBody, {
  1357	    previousFrontmatter: frontmatter,
  1358	    expectedRaw: doc.raw,
  1359	    expectedMtime: doc.mtime,
  1360	    beforeCommit,
  1361	  });
  1362	  return { filePath, frontmatter: nextFm, mintedUid };
  1363	}
  1364	
  1365	/**
  1768	      + 'The vault is unchanged. Re-read those documents and run this again.',
  1769	  );
  1770	}
  1771	
  1772	export function writeFileAtomically(filePath, text, options = {}) {
  1773	  const temporaryPath = `${filePath}.oatlas-tmp-${process.pid}`;
  1774	  const existingMode = existingRegularFileMode(filePath);
  1775	  let descriptor = null;
  1776	  try {
  1777	    descriptor = openSync(temporaryPath, 'wx');
  1778	    // Applied before the contents so the temp file never widens a private original's mode, even briefly.
  1779	    if (existingMode !== null) fchmodSync(descriptor, existingMode);
  1780	    writeFileSync(descriptor, text, 'utf-8');
  1781	  // Flush to disk before the rename — otherwise power can be lost with the new
  1782	  // name in place and the contents still in cache.
  1783	    fsyncSync(descriptor);
  1784	    closeSync(descriptor);
  1785	    descriptor = null;
  1786	    options.beforeCommit?.();
  1787	    if (entryChangedOnDisk({
  1788	      path: filePath,
  1789	      expectedRaw: options.expectedRaw,
  1790	      expectedAbsent: options.expectedAbsent,
  1791	    })) {
  1792	      if (options.conflictSlug) {
  1793	        throw new VaultConflictError(
  1794	          options.conflictSlug,
  1795	          options.expectedMtime,
  1796	          getFileMtime(filePath),
  1797	        );
  1798	      }
  1799	      throw changedOnDiskError([filePath]);
  1800	    }
  1801	    renameSync(temporaryPath, filePath);
  1802	  } finally {
  1803	    if (descriptor !== null) {
  1804	      try {
  1805	        closeSync(descriptor);
  1875	export function applyAllOrNothing(plan, options = {}) {
  1876	  if (!Array.isArray(plan) || plan.length === 0) return { applied: 0 };
  1877	
  1878	  /*
  1879	   * ⓪ **A plan that writes and then deletes the same file drops the delete.**
  1880	   *
  1881	   * A rename differing only in case produces exactly this plan:
  1882	   *   write `capabilities/auth.md` · delete `capabilities/Auth.md`
  1883	   * and the macOS and Windows filesystems treat those as the **same file**, so
  1884	   * deleting after writing deletes what was just written — the node disappears
  1885	   * entirely and the tool reports success (reproduced in the 2026-08-16 review).
  1886	   *
  1887	   * The front door (`rename_concept`) rejects that case, but three tools build
  1888	   * this plan (rename, merge, reclassify), so the **write layer** blocks it too.
  1889	   * What a string comparison cannot catch is caught here by **real path**.
  1890	   */
  1891	  const writeTargets = new Set();
  1892	  for (const entry of plan) {
  1893	    if (entry.op !== 'write') continue;
  1894	    writeTargets.add(sameFileKey(entry.path));
  1895	  }
  1896	  const safePlan = plan.filter(
  1897	    (entry) => entry.op !== 'delete' || !writeTargets.has(sameFileKey(entry.path)),
  1898	  );
  1899	  if (safePlan.length !== plan.length) plan = safePlan;
  1900	
  1901	  if (options.requireRevisions === true) {
  1902	    const missingRevisions = plan
  1903	      .filter((entry) => {
  1904	        if (existsSync(entry.path)) return entry.expectedRaw === undefined;
  1905	        if (entry.op === 'write') return entry.expectedAbsent !== true;
  1906	        return entry.expectedRaw === undefined;
  1907	      })
  1908	      .map((entry) => entry.path);
  1909	    if (missingRevisions.length > 0) {
  1910	      throw new Error(
  1911	        `Refused before writing anything: ${missingRevisions.length} plan item(s) are missing snapshot revision data:\n  `
  1912	          + `${missingRevisions.join('\n  ')}\nThe vault is unchanged. Rebuild the plan from fresh reads.`,
  1913	      );
  1914	    }
  1915	  }
  1916	
  1917	  /*
  1918	   * ⓪-b **If someone edited it in the meantime, write nothing at all.**
  1919	   *
  1920	   * Why (review 2026-08-16): the `expected_mtime` check existed only on the
  1921	   * single-file paths. But the three tools using this function (rename, merge,
  1922	   * reclassify) rewrite N referencing documents from **a snapshot read minutes
  1923	   * earlier**. If the user edited one of them in Obsidian in between, that edit
  1924	   * vanished silently — and a human and an agent sharing one folder is the exact
  1925	   * situation this product sells, so protection was missing precisely there.
  1926	   *
  1927	   * When the planner attaches `expectedMtime` to an entry, it is checked here.
  1928	   * Without it nothing is checked, as before (zero regression) — but that slot is
  1929	   * now "not supplied" rather than "does not exist".
  1930	   */
  1931	  const conflicts = [];
  1932	  for (const entry of plan) {
  1933	    if (entryChangedOnDisk(entry)) conflicts.push(entry.path);
  1934	  }
  1935	  if (conflicts.length > 0) {
  1936	    throw changedOnDiskError(conflicts);
  1937	  }
  1938	
  1939	  // ① Pre-check, before a single character is written. The common failures
  1940	  //    (read-only file, locked file, read-only vault) are caught here, so no
  1941	  //    rollback is needed at all.
  1942	  const blocked = [];
  1943	  for (const entry of plan) {
  1944	    const dir = dirname(entry.path);
  1945	    try {
  1946	      if (existsSync(entry.path)) {
  1947	        accessSync(entry.path, fsConstants.W_OK);
  1948	      } else if (entry.op === 'write') {
  1949	        accessSync(nearestExistingParent(entry.path), fsConstants.W_OK);
  1950	      }
  2048	 * Rewrites every frontmatter array key and body link that points at `targetSlug`
  2049	 * to `nextSlug`. The core operation behind rename_concept and merge_concepts.
  2050	 *
  2051	 * Matching policy (same as findBacklinks):
  2052	 *  - absolute slug (`capabilities/mcp-server`)
  2053	 *  - last segment (`mcp-server`) — the replacement uses `nextSlug`'s tail rather
  2054	 *    than keeping the target tail, so a rename shows the new name consistently
  2055	 *    whichever form the slug was written in
  2056	 *  - trailing match (`…/mcp-server`) follows the same policy
  2057	 *
  2058	 * Body replacement covers `[[targetSlug]]` and `(targetSlug.md)`.
  2059	 *
  2060	 * `options.dryRun = true` previews without writing to disk.
  2061	 * `options.excludeSlugs` lets the caller skip documents it replaces in the same plan.
  2062	 *
  2063	 * Returns `{ updates: [{ slug, beforeKeys, afterKeys, bodyHit }], totalUpdated }`.
  2064	 */
  2065	export function redirectBacklinks(rootPath, targetSlug, nextSlug, options = {}) {
  2066	  /**
  2067	   * With `deferWrite: true` only the plan is built and returned as `plan`; disk is
  2068	   * untouched. Different from `dryRun`: a dry run is a preview *for the user*,
  2069	   * while this lets **the caller merge its own writes into one plan** and apply
  2070	   * them all-or-nothing. `rename_concept` uses it to bind file creation, backlink
  2071	   * rewriting, and old-file deletion into one unit.
  2072	   */
  2073	  const { dryRun = false, deferWrite = false, excludeSlugs = [] } = options;
  2074	  const excluded = new Set(Array.isArray(excludeSlugs) ? excludeSlugs : []);
  2075	  if (typeof targetSlug !== 'string' || !targetSlug) {
  2076	    throw new Error('targetSlug is required.');
  2077	  }
  2078	  if (typeof nextSlug !== 'string' || !nextSlug) {
  2079	    throw new Error('nextSlug is required.');
  2080	  }
  2081	  if (targetSlug === nextSlug) {
  2082	    return { updates: [], totalUpdated: 0, plan: [] };
  2083	  }
  2084	
  2085	  const docs = loadVaultDocs(rootPath);
  2086	  const targetTail = targetSlug.split('/').pop();
  2087	  const nextTail = nextSlug.split('/').pop();
  2088	  const tailMatches = docs
  2089	    .map((doc) => doc.slug)
  2090	    .filter((slug) => slug.split('/').pop() === targetTail);
  2091	  // A bare/suffix tail is shorthand only while it uniquely resolves. When
  2092	  // capabilities/foo and elements/foo both exist, rewriting "foo" would
  2093	  // silently redirect whichever concept the author meant and can even mutate
  2094	  // the other node's frontmatter slug. Exact canonical refs remain safe.
  2095	  const canRewriteTail = tailMatches.length === 1 && tailMatches[0] === targetSlug;
  2096	
  2097	  function rewriteArrayItem(value) {
  2098	    if (typeof value !== 'string') return { value, changed: false };
  2099	    if (value === targetSlug) return { value: nextSlug, changed: true };
  2100	    if (canRewriteTail && value === targetTail) return { value: nextTail, changed: true };
  2101	    if (canRewriteTail && value.endsWith(`/${targetTail}`)) {
  2102	      // path-prefixed tail — preserved prefix plus the new tail
  2103	      const prefix = value.slice(0, value.length - targetTail.length);
  2104	      return { value: `${prefix}${nextTail}`, changed: true };
  2105	    }
  2106	    return { value, changed: false };
  2107	  }
  2108	
  2109	  const updates = [];
  2110	  /** The write plan destined for disk: applied in one go once the loop ends. */
  2111	  const plan = [];
  2112	  for (const doc of docs) {
  2113	    if (doc.slug === targetSlug || excluded.has(doc.slug)) continue;
  2114	    const filePath = slugToPath(rootPath, doc.slug);
  2115	    const nextFm = { ...doc.frontmatter };
  2116	    const beforeKeys = [];
  2117	    const afterKeys = [];
  2118	    let fmChanged = false;
  2119	
  2120	    for (const key of Object.keys(nextFm)) {
  2121	      const value = nextFm[key];
  2122	      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  2123	        const before = [...value];
  2124	        const after = value.map((v) => rewriteArrayItem(v).value);
  2125	        if (before.some((b, i) => b !== after[i])) {
  2126	          // dedup + sort — never append a duplicate when nextSlug is already
  2127	          // present, so the same graph state leaves the same frontmatter array.
  2128	          const deduped = normalizeRelationRefs(after);
  2129	          nextFm[key] = deduped;
  2130	          beforeKeys.push({ key, before });
  2131	          afterKeys.push({ key, after: deduped });
  2132	          fmChanged = true;
  2133	        }
  2134	      } else if (typeof value === 'string') {
  2135	        // Only graph reference slots are rewritten (`domain:` plus the
  2136	        // GRAPH_ARRAY_KEYS family). An evidence string such as `path:` is not a
  2137	        // reference — measured 2026-08-01 while flattening the dogfood vault: the
  2138	        // tail-suffix clause of the rename `elements/src/widgets/docs-vault` →
  2139	        // `elements/docs-vault-widget` also rewrote **another node's**
  2140	        // `path: src/entities/docs-vault` to `…/docs-vault-widget`, pointing it at
  2141	        // a file that does not exist (3 cases of pathDrift).
  2142	        const isRefSlot = key === 'domain' || GRAPH_ARRAY_KEY_SET.has(key);
  2143	        const r = isRefSlot ? rewriteArrayItem(value) : { changed: false };
  2144	        if (r.changed) {
  2145	          nextFm[key] = r.value;
  2146	          beforeKeys.push({ key, before: value });
  2147	          afterKeys.push({ key, after: r.value });
  2148	          fmChanged = true;
  2149	        }
  2150	      } else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
  2151	        // The KEY of an object map value (`relation_notes: {ref: "why"}`) is a
  2152	        // rename target too. Without this, a relation's rationale note is orphaned
  2153	        // the moment the rename happens — the red-team finding that blocked the
  2154	        // schema from shipping.
  2155	        //
  2156	        // Key-collision merge policy: when both the old and the new key exist, the
  2157	        // existing (new) value wins — a note the user already wrote under the new
  2158	        // name is the more recent intent, and letting the rename overwrite it is
  2159	        // silent data loss. The displaced old value is not discarded: it stays in
  2160	        // beforeKeys, visible in the dry run and the audit.
  2161	        const entries = Object.entries(value);
  2162	        let mapChanged = false;
  2163	        const nextMap = {};
  2164	        for (const [mapKey, mapValue] of entries) {
  2165	          const r = rewriteArrayItem(mapKey);
  2166	          if (!r.changed) {
  2167	            if (!(mapKey in nextMap)) nextMap[mapKey] = mapValue;
  2168	            continue;
  2169	          }
  2170	          mapChanged = true;
  2171	          if (r.value in nextMap || entries.some(([k]) => k === r.value)) {
  2172	            // Collision — the existing (new key) value wins; the old value is only recorded.
  2173	            continue;
  2174	          }
  2175	          nextMap[r.value] = mapValue;
  2176	        }
  2177	        if (mapChanged) {
  2178	          // Preserve the collision winner (the original new key) value
  2179	          for (const [mapKey, mapValue] of entries) {
  2180	            if (!(mapKey in nextMap) && !rewriteArrayItem(mapKey).changed) nextMap[mapKey] = mapValue;
  2181	          }
  2182	          beforeKeys.push({ key, before: value });
  2183	          afterKeys.push({ key, after: nextMap });
  2184	          nextFm[key] = nextMap;
  2185	          fmChanged = true;
  2186	        }
  2187	      }
  2188	    }
  2189	
  2190	    let nextBody = doc.body;
  2191	    let bodyChanged = false;
  2192	    if (nextBody.includes(`[[${targetSlug}]]`)) {
  2193	      nextBody = nextBody.split(`[[${targetSlug}]]`).join(`[[${nextSlug}]]`);
  2194	      bodyChanged = true;
  2195	    }
  2196	    if (canRewriteTail && nextBody.includes(`[[${targetTail}]]`)) {
  2197	      nextBody = nextBody.split(`[[${targetTail}]]`).join(`[[${nextTail}]]`);
  2198	      bodyChanged = true;
  2199	    }
  2200	    if (nextBody.includes(`(${targetSlug}.md)`)) {
  2201	      nextBody = nextBody.split(`(${targetSlug}.md)`).join(`(${nextSlug}.md)`);
  2202	      bodyChanged = true;
  2203	    }
  2204	
  2205	    if (!fmChanged && !bodyChanged) continue;
  2206	
  2207	    updates.push({
  2208	      slug: doc.slug,
  2209	      title: docTitle(doc),
  2210	      beforeKeys,
  2211	      afterKeys,
  2212	      bodyChanged,
  2213	    });
  2214	
  2215	    // **Do not write here.** This line used to be a `writeFileSync`, so a failure
  2216	    // on the next file left a half vault with only the earlier ones changed.
  2217	    plan.push({
  2218	      op: 'write',
  2219	      path: filePath,
  2220	      content: buildMarkdown({ frontmatter: nextFm, body: nextBody }),
  2221	      /*
  2222	       * **Carry the read timestamp along** (review 2026-08-16).
  2223	       *
  2224	       * This document is a snapshot read seconds or minutes ago. If the user
  2225	       * edited the file in their own editor in between, writing it as-is here
  2226	       * destroys that edit. Compare this value against disk immediately before
  2227	       * the write.
  2228	       */
  2229	      expectedMtime: doc.mtime,
  2230	      expectedRaw: doc.raw,
  2231	    });
  2232	  }
  2233	
  2234	  if (!dryRun && !deferWrite) applyAllOrNothing(plan, { requireRevisions: true });
  2235	
  2236	  return {
  2237	    updates,
  2238	    totalUpdated: updates.length,
  2239	    ...(deferWrite ? { plan } : {}),
  2240	  };
  2508	        limit: {
  2509	          type: 'integer',
  2510	          minimum: 1,
  2511	          maximum: 100,
  2512	          description: 'Maximum newest-first vault commits to return. Defaults to 20; maximum 100.',
  2513	        },
  2514	      },
  2515	    },
  2516	    outputSchema: GIT_HISTORY_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,
  2517	  },
  2518	  {
  2519	    name: 'git_snapshot',
  2520	    description:
  2521	      'Create a local, vault-scoped Git checkpoint. Dry-run by default and returns exact expectedHead, files, validation, risk, and the shared previewReady/canConfirm/wouldChange/blockedReasons safety contract. confirm:true requires that expectedHead, blocks validator errors and Git operations in progress, commits only the vault pathspec, leaves outside files untouched, and never pushes.',
  2522	    inputSchema: {
  2523	      type: 'object',
  2524	      properties: {
  2525	        confirm: {
  2526	          type: 'boolean',
  2527	          description: 'Default false. Set true only after reviewing the dry-run preview and its risk/validation fields.',
  2528	        },
  2529	        expectedHead: nonBlankStringSchema(
  2530	          'Required with confirm:true. Copy the exact expectedHead returned by the immediately preceding dry-run; this prevents committing after a concurrent HEAD change.',
  2531	        ),
  2532	        message: nonBlankStringSchema(
  2533	          'Optional local commit subject, one line and at most 200 characters. A deterministic ontology snapshot subject is generated when omitted.',
  2534	          { maxLength: 200, pattern: '^[^\\r\\n]+$' },
  2535	        ),
  2536	      },
  2537	    },
  2538	    outputSchema: GIT_SNAPSHOT_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,
  2539	  },
  2540	  {
  2940	    },
  2941	  },
  2942	  {
  2943	    name: 'finalize_project_meaning',
  2944	    description:
  2945	      'Finalize the current project competency Markdown after concept/relation writes, vault validation, and a complete project compile. ' +
  2946	      'The server derives the current body digest, project graph hash, source fingerprint, and witness inventory itself; callers cannot submit or restamp those values. ' +

  2949	    inputSchema: {

  2956	          type: 'number',

  2991	  {
  2992	    name: 'connect_project_source',

  6117	const READ_TOOL_NAMES = new Set([
  6118	  'connection_info',
  6119	  'git_status',
  6120	  'git_history',
  6121	  'list_concepts',

  6139	const DESTRUCTIVE_TOOL_NAMES = new Set([
  6140	  'git_snapshot',
  6141	  // Removes a measured binding + its receipt. Reversible only by re-measuring.
  6142	  'disconnect_project_source',
  6143	  'delete_concept',

  6151	  // rewrite of a file outside the vault).
  6152	  'absorb_document',
  6153	]);

  6155	const IDEMPOTENT_TOOL_NAMES = new Set([
  6156	  'add_relation',
  6157	  'add_relations',
  6158	  'remove_relation',
  6328	}
