  6330	// ── Tool handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  6331	
  6332	server.setRequestHandler('tools/call', async (request) => {
  6333	  const { name } = request.params;
  6334	  try {
  6335	    // Read-only guard — reject any known write tool even if the caller has a
  6336	    // stale tools/list that still shows it. Unknown names fall through to the
  6337	    // normal unknown-tool error below.
  6338	    if (READ_ONLY_MODE && TOOL_BY_NAME.has(name) && !READ_TOOL_NAMES.has(name)) {
  6339	      throw new Error(
  6340	        `Tool "${name}" is unavailable: server is in read-only mode (OATLAS_READ_ONLY). Only read tools are exposed.`,
  6341	      );
  6342	    }
  6343	    const args = normalizeToolArguments(request.params.arguments, name);
  6344	
  6345	    // ── The write checkpoint ──
  6346	    // Every tool that is not a read tool passes a human decision first when the
  6347	    // launcher turned the gate on. It sits **before** the switch so a tool added later
  6348	    // is covered by being outside the read set, not by someone remembering to guard it.
  6349	    if (WRITE_CONSENT_MODE && TOOL_BY_NAME.has(name) && !READ_TOOL_NAMES.has(name)) {
  6350	      const consent = await requestWriteConsent({
  6351	        server,
  6352	        toolName: name,
  6353	        args,
  6354	        enabled: true,
  6355	      });
  6356	      if (!consent.allowed) {
  6357	        // A refusal is a normal outcome, not a crash: the agent is told plainly
  6358	        // that nothing changed and why, so it can report back instead of retrying.
  6359	        const error = new Error(consent.message);
  6360	        error.code = consent.reason;
  7578	function gitHistoryTool({ limit = 20 } = {}) {
  7579	  requireOptionalPositiveInteger(limit, 'limit', { max: 100 });
  7580	  return inspectVaultGitHistory({
  7581	    repoRoot: REPO_ROOT,
  7582	    vaultRoot: VAULT_ROOT,
  7583	    limit,
  7584	  });
  7585	}
  7586	
  7587	function gitSnapshotTool({ confirm = false, expectedHead, message } = {}) {
  7588	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
  7589	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(expectedHead, 'expectedHead');
  7590	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(message, 'message');
  7591	  if (message !== undefined && (message.length > 200 || /[\r\n]/.test(message))) {
  7592	    throw new Error('message must be one line and at most 200 characters.');
  7593	  }
  7594	
  7595	  const report = validateVaultTool();
  7596	  const validation = {
  7597	    scanned: report.scanned,
  7598	    problemFiles: report.summary.problemFiles,
  7599	    errorFiles: report.summary.errorFiles,
  7600	    warningFiles: report.summary.warningFiles,
  7601	    pathDrifts: report.pathDrift.drifts.length,
  7602	  };
  7603	  if (confirm && validation.errorFiles > 0) {
  7604	    throw new Error(
  7605	      `git_snapshot blocked: validate_vault found ${validation.errorFiles} file(s) with errors. Repair them and run a new dry-run.`,
  7606	    );
  7607	  }
  7608	
  7609	  const result = snapshotVaultGit({
  7610	    repoRoot: REPO_ROOT,
  7611	    vaultRoot: VAULT_ROOT,
  7612	    confirm,
  7613	    expectedHead,
  7614	    message,
  7615	  });
  7616	  const validationBlocker =
  7617	    validation.errorFiles > 0
  7618	      ? `validate_vault reports ${validation.errorFiles} file(s) with errors; repair them before confirmation`
  7619	      : null;
  7620	  const blockedReasons = [
  7621	    ...(Array.isArray(result.blockedReasons) ? result.blockedReasons : []),
  7622	    ...(validationBlocker ? [validationBlocker] : []),
  7623	  ];
  7624	  const guardedResult = {
  7625	    ...result,
  7626	    canConfirm: result.canConfirm === true && !validationBlocker,
  7627	    blockedReasons: [...new Set(blockedReasons)],
  7628	  };
  7780	  };
  7781	}
  7782	
  7783	function removeRelation({ from, to, type, confirm = false, expected_mtime }) {
  7784	  requireNonBlankString(from, 'from');
  7785	  requireNonBlankString(to, 'to');
  7786	  requireNonBlankString(type, 'type');
  7787	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
  7788	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
  7789	  const key = RELATION_KEY[type];
  7790	  if (!key) throw new Error(formatAllowedValueError('type', type, RELATION_TYPES));
  7791	  const canonicalFrom = resolveExistingVaultSlug(from);
  7792	  const canonicalTo = resolveExistingVaultSlug(to);
  7793	  if (!canonicalFrom) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Source slug does not exist in vault', from));
  7794	  if (!canonicalTo) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Target slug does not exist in vault', to));
  7795	  const doc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalFrom));
  7796	  if (typeof expected_mtime === 'number' && doc.mtime !== expected_mtime) {
  7797	    throw new VaultConflictError(canonicalFrom, expected_mtime, doc.mtime);
  7798	  }
  7799	  const exists = relationExists(doc, key, canonicalTo);
  7800	  const notes = doc.frontmatter.relation_notes && typeof doc.frontmatter.relation_notes === 'object'
  7801	    ? { ...doc.frontmatter.relation_notes }
  7802	    : {};
  7803	  const matchingNoteKeys = matchingRelationNoteKeys(notes, canonicalTo);
  7804	  const removedRationale = matchingNoteKeys
  7805	    .map((key) => notes[key])
  7806	    .find((value) => typeof value === 'string');
  7807	  const dryRun = !confirm;
  7808	  const base = {
  7809	    ok: exists,
  7810	    dryRun,
  7811	    changed: false,
  7812	    ...destructivePreviewState({
  7813	      dryRun,
  7814	      wouldChange: exists,
  7815	      blockedReasons: exists ? [] : ['relation does not exist; confirmation would be a no-op'],
  7816	    }),
  7817	    exists,
  7818	    from: canonicalFrom,
  7819	    to: canonicalTo,
  7820	    type,
  7821	    key,
  7822	    ...(removedRationale ? { removedRationale } : {}),
  7823	  };
  7824	  if (!exists || !confirm) return base;
  7825	  const patch = key === 'domain'
  7826	    ? { domain: null }
  7827	    : { [key]: doc.frontmatter[key].filter((ref) => !relationRefMatches(ref, canonicalTo)) };
  7828	  for (const noteKey of matchingNoteKeys) delete notes[noteKey];
  7829	  patch.relation_notes = Object.keys(notes).length > 0 ? notes : null;
  7830	  patchFrontmatter(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalFrom, patch, { expectedMtime: expected_mtime });
  7831	  return { ...base, ok: true, dryRun: false, changed: true, postWriteMaintenance: compactPostWriteMaintenance() };
  7832	}
  7833	
  7834	function replaceRelation({ from, oldTo, oldType, newTo, newType, why, confirm = false, expected_mtime }) {
  7835	  for (const [value, name] of [[from, 'from'], [oldTo, 'oldTo'], [oldType, 'oldType'], [newTo, 'newTo'], [newType, 'newType']]) requireNonBlankString(value, name);
  7836	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
  7837	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
  7838	  const oldKey = RELATION_KEY[oldType];
  7839	  const newKey = RELATION_KEY[newType];
  7840	  if (!oldKey) throw new Error(formatAllowedValueError('oldType', oldType, RELATION_TYPES));
  7841	  if (!newKey) throw new Error(formatAllowedValueError('newType', newType, RELATION_TYPES));
  7842	  const canonicalFrom = resolveExistingVaultSlug(from);
  7843	  const canonicalOldTo = resolveExistingVaultSlug(oldTo);
  7844	  const canonicalNewTo = resolveExistingVaultSlug(newTo);
  7845	  if (!canonicalFrom) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Source slug does not exist in vault', from));
  7846	  if (!canonicalOldTo) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Old target slug does not exist in vault', oldTo));
  7847	  if (!canonicalNewTo) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('New target slug does not exist in vault', newTo));
  7848	  const doc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalFrom));
  7849	  if (typeof expected_mtime === 'number' && doc.mtime !== expected_mtime) throw new VaultConflictError(canonicalFrom, expected_mtime, doc.mtime);
  7850	  if (!relationExists(doc, oldKey, canonicalOldTo)) throw new Error(`Relation does not exist: ${canonicalFrom} --${oldType}--> ${canonicalOldTo}.`);
  7851	  const oldRelation = { to: canonicalOldTo, type: oldType, key: oldKey };
  7852	  const newRelation = { to: canonicalNewTo, type: newType, key: newKey };
  7853	  const dryRun = !confirm;
  7854	  const base = {
  7855	    ok: false,
  7856	    dryRun,
  7857	    changed: false,
  7858	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun, wouldChange: true }),
  7859	    from: canonicalFrom,
  7860	    oldRelation,
  7861	    newRelation,
  7862	  };
  7863	  if (!confirm) return base;
  7864	  const patch = {};
  7865	  if (oldKey === 'domain') patch.domain = null;
  7866	  else patch[oldKey] = (doc.frontmatter[oldKey] || [])
  7867	    .filter((ref) => !relationRefMatches(ref, canonicalOldTo));
  7868	  if (newKey === 'domain') patch.domain = canonicalNewTo;
  8008	    postWriteMaintenance: results.some((row) => row.ok && row.changed !== false)
  8009	      ? compactPostWriteMaintenance()
  8010	      : undefined,
  8011	  };
  8012	}
  8013	
  8014	function patchConcept({ slug, frontmatter, body, expected_mtime }) {
  8015	  requireNonBlankString(slug, 'slug');
  8016	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
  8017	  if (frontmatter === undefined && body === undefined) {
  8018	    throw new Error('At least one of `frontmatter` or `body` is required.');
  8019	  }
  8020	  requireOptionalPlainObject(frontmatter, 'frontmatter');
  8021	  requireValidFrontmatterPatch(frontmatter);
  8022	  if (body !== undefined && typeof body !== 'string') {
  8023	    throw new Error('body must be a string.');
  8024	  }
  8025	  // A patch that includes `title` forces a non-empty string. The UI's
  8026	  // renameVaultDoc rejects blanks; leaving MCP open lets an agent's slip create an
  8027	  // untitled node and drift the ontology. `null` is separate — it means "delete
  8028	  // the key" — and deleting `title` itself breaks the frontmatter.
  8029	  if (frontmatter !== undefined && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(frontmatter, 'title')) {
  8030	    const t = frontmatter.title;
  8031	    if (t === null) {
  8032	      throw new Error('title cannot be deleted from a vault node — pass a new non-empty string instead.');
  8033	    }
  8034	    if (!isValidVaultTitle(t)) {
  8035	      throw new Error('title must be a non-empty string.');
  8036	    }
  8037	  }
  8038	  const { filePath, mintedUid } = updateDoc(VAULT_ROOT, slug, {
  8039	    frontmatter,
  8040	    body,
  8041	    expectedMtime: typeof expected_mtime === 'number' ? expected_mtime : undefined,
  8042	  });
  8043	  return {
  8044	    ok: true,
  8045	    slug,
  8046	    filePath,
  8047	    changed: true,
  8048	    // If this write gave identity to a hand-authored node (one created in an
  8725	}
  8726	
  8727	function connectProjectSourceTool({ projectSlug, rootPath, confirm, repair } = {}) {
  8728	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(projectSlug, 'projectSlug');
  8729	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(rootPath, 'rootPath');
  8730	  if (typeof projectSlug !== 'string') throw new Error('projectSlug is required.');
  8731	  if (typeof rootPath === 'string' && !isAbsolute(rootPath)) {
  8732	    throw new Error(`rootPath must be an absolute local path; received "${rootPath}".`);
  8733	  }
  8734	
  8735	  const allDocs = loadVaultDocs(VAULT_ROOT);
  8736	  const canonicalSlug = resolveProjectNodeSlug(projectSlug, allDocs);
  8737	  const artifact = COMPILED_ONTOLOGY_CACHE.get({ includeIndexes: true });
  8738	  const { docs, graphHash } = projectSourceScope(artifact, canonicalSlug, allDocs);
  8739	  if (!graphHash) {
  8740	    throw new Error(
  8741	      `connect_project_source blocked: the project scope for "${canonicalSlug}" is incomplete, so no receipt could detect later ontology drift. Repair the project containment first (query_ontology({operation:"project_scope", project:"${canonicalSlug}"})).`,
  8742	    );
  8743	  }
  8744	  const witnesses = deriveProjectSourceWitnessesFromDocs({ projectSlug: canonicalSlug, docs });
  8745	
  8746	  const sidecar = readProjectSourceBindings(VAULT_ROOT);
  8747	  if (sidecar.status === 'malformed' && repair !== true) {
  8748	    throw new Error(
  8749	      `connect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is malformed. Inspect it, then re-run with repair: true to discard and rewrite it.`,
  8750	    );
  8751	  }
  8752	  const bound = sidecar.status === 'ok'
  8753	    ? sidecar.bindings.filter((binding) => binding.projectSlug === canonicalSlug)
  8754	    : [];
  8755	
  8756	  let inference = null;
  8757	  let selectedRoot = null;
  8758	  let mode = bound.length > 0 ? 'replace' : 'connect';
  8759	  if (typeof rootPath === 'string') {
  8760	    selectedRoot = rootPath;
  8761	  } else if (bound.length === 1) {
  8762	    selectedRoot = bound[0].rootPath;
  8763	    mode = 'remeasure';
  8764	  } else {
  8765	    const { vaultRootPath, candidates } = collectProjectSourceCandidates(VAULT_ROOT);
  8766	    inference = inferProjectSourceProposal({ vaultRootPath, candidates });
  8767	    if (inference.status !== 'proposed') {
  8768	      throw new Error(
  8769	        'connect_project_source found no enclosing code folder for this vault (no git repository and no project manifest above it). '
  8770	        + 'Pass rootPath with the absolute folder that holds the code this ontology describes.',
  8771	      );
  8772	    }
  8773	    selectedRoot = inference.candidate.rootPath;
  8774	    inference = { ...inference, candidates };
  8775	  }
  8776	
  8777	  let probe;
  8778	  try {
  8779	    probe = inspectProjectSource(selectedRoot);
  8780	  } catch (err) {
  8781	    throw new Error(
  8782	      `connect_project_source could not measure "${selectedRoot}": ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`,
  8783	    );
  8784	  }
  8785	
  8786	  const measuredAt = new Date().toISOString();
  8787	  const receipt = buildProjectSourceReceipt({
  8788	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8789	    graphHash,
  8790	    probe,
  8791	    witnesses,
  8792	    measuredAt,
  8793	  });
  8794	  if (inference) {
  8795	    inference = inferProjectSourceProposal({
  8796	      vaultRootPath: VAULT_ROOT,
  8797	      candidates: inference.candidates,
  8798	      witnessSummary: receipt.witnessSummary,
  8799	    });
  8800	  }
  8801	
  8802	  const binding = {
  8803	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8804	    sourceId: probe.sourceId,
  8805	    rootPath: probe.rootPath,
  8806	    kind: probe.kind,
  8807	    boundAt: measuredAt,
  8808	  };
  8809	  const bindingView = {
  8810	    rootPath: probe.rootPath,
  8811	    kind: probe.kind,
  8812	    sourceId: probe.sourceId,
  8813	    dirty: probe.dirty,
  8814	    truncated: probe.truncated,
  8815	    inventoryFiles: probe.files.length,
  8816	  };
  8817	
  8818	  if (confirm !== true) {
  8819	    return {
  8820	      ok: true,
  8821	      changed: false,
  8822	      confirmed: false,
  8823	      contract: PROJECT_SOURCE_CONNECT_CONTRACT,
  8824	      projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8825	      mode,
  8826	      binding: bindingView,
  8827	      inference,
  8828	      previewReceipt: receipt,
  8829	      previousBindingCount: bound.length,
  8830	      nextCall: {
  8831	        tool: 'connect_project_source',
  8832	        arguments: { projectSlug: canonicalSlug, rootPath: probe.rootPath, confirm: true },
  8833	      },
  8834	      undo: undoPlan(canonicalSlug),
  8835	    };
  8836	  }
  8837	
  8838	  const written = writeProjectSourceBinding(VAULT_ROOT, { ...binding, receipt }, { repair: repair === true });
  8839	  if (written.status === 'blocked_unsafe_path') {
  8840	    throw new Error(
  8841	      `connect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is behind an unsafe sidecar path. repair: true cannot bypass a symlink or junction boundary.`,
  8842	    );
  8843	  }
  8844	  if (written.status === 'blocked_malformed') {
  8845	    throw new Error(
  8846	      `connect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is malformed. Re-run with repair: true to discard and rewrite it.`,
  8847	    );
  8848	  }
  8849	  if (written.status !== 'written') {
  8850	    throw new Error(
  8851	      `connect_project_source could not persist the binding (${written.status}).`,
  8852	    );
  8853	  }
  8854	  const projectSource = readProjectSourceView(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalSlug, graphHash);
  8855	  return {
  8856	    ok: true,
  8857	    changed: true,
  8858	    confirmed: true,
  8859	    contract: PROJECT_SOURCE_CONNECT_CONTRACT,
  8860	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8861	    mode,
  8862	    binding: bindingView,
  8863	    inference,
  8864	    projectSource,
  8865	    remedy: projectSourceRemedy(projectSource),
  8866	    previousBindingCount: bound.length,
  8867	    undo: undoPlan(canonicalSlug),
  8868	  };
  8869	}
  8870	
  8871	function disconnectProjectSourceTool({ projectSlug, confirm } = {}) {
  8872	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(projectSlug, 'projectSlug');
  8873	  if (typeof projectSlug !== 'string') throw new Error('projectSlug is required.');
  8874	
  8875	  const allDocs = loadVaultDocs(VAULT_ROOT);
  8876	  // A binding can outlive its project node. Disconnect must still be able to
  8877	  // clear it, so an unresolvable slug falls back to the literal value.
  8878	  const canonicalSlug = resolveExistingVaultSlug(projectSlug, allDocs) ?? projectSlug;
  8879	  const sidecar = readProjectSourceBindings(VAULT_ROOT);
  8880	  if (sidecar.status === 'unsafe_path') {
  8881	    throw new Error(
  8882	      `disconnect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is behind an unsafe sidecar path. Replace the symlink or junction with a real vault-local directory first.`,
  8883	    );
  8884	  }
  8885	  if (sidecar.status === 'malformed') {
  8886	    throw new Error(
  8887	      `disconnect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is malformed. Inspect it by hand, or re-connect with repair: true to rewrite it.`,
  8888	    );
  8889	  }
  8890	  const bound = sidecar.status === 'ok'
  8891	    ? sidecar.bindings.filter((binding) => binding.projectSlug === canonicalSlug)
  8892	    : [];
  8893	  const removable = bound.map((binding) => ({
  8894	    rootPath: binding.rootPath,
  8895	    kind: binding.kind,
  8896	    boundAt: binding.boundAt,
  8897	    measuredAt: binding.receipt?.measuredAt ?? null,
  8898	  }));
  8899	
  8900	  if (confirm !== true) {
  8901	    return {
  8902	      ok: true,
  8903	      changed: false,
  8904	      confirmed: false,
  8905	      contract: PROJECT_SOURCE_DISCONNECT_CONTRACT,
  8906	      projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8907	      removed: 0,
  8908	      bindings: removable,
  8909	      nextCall: {
  8910	        tool: 'disconnect_project_source',
  8911	        arguments: { projectSlug: canonicalSlug, confirm: true },
  8912	      },
  8913	    };
  8914	  }
  8915	
  8916	  const result = removeProjectSourceBindings(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalSlug);
  8917	  if (result.status === 'blocked_unsafe_path') {
  8918	    throw new Error(
  8919	      `disconnect_project_source blocked: ${PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_RELATIVE_PATH} is behind an unsafe sidecar path.`,
  8920	    );
  8921	  }
  8922	  if (result.status === 'persistence_failed') {
  8923	    throw new Error('disconnect_project_source could not persist the sidecar update.');
  8924	  }
  8925	  const projectSource = readProjectSourceView(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalSlug, undefined);
  8926	  return {
  8927	    ok: true,
  8928	    changed: result.removed > 0,
  8929	    confirmed: true,
  8930	    contract: PROJECT_SOURCE_DISCONNECT_CONTRACT,
  8935	    remedy: projectSourceRemedy(projectSource),
  8936	  };
  8937	}
  8938	
  8939	function finalizeProjectMeaningTool({ projectSlug, expected_mtime } = {}) {
  8940	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(projectSlug, 'projectSlug');
  8941	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
  8942	  if (typeof projectSlug !== 'string') throw new Error('projectSlug is required.');
  8943	  if (typeof expected_mtime !== 'number') throw new Error('expected_mtime is required.');
  8944	
  8945	  const allDocs = loadVaultDocs(VAULT_ROOT);
  8946	  const canonicalSlug = resolveExistingVaultSlug(projectSlug, allDocs);
  8947	  if (!canonicalSlug) {
  8948	    throw new Error(
  8949	      `Project slug does not exist in vault: "${projectSlug}". Use list_concepts({kind:"project"}) to choose an exact project slug.`,
  8950	    );
  8951	  }
  8952	  const projectDoc = allDocs.find((doc) => doc.slug === canonicalSlug);
  8953	  if (projectDoc?.frontmatter?.kind !== 'project') {
  8954	    throw new Error(`finalize_project_meaning requires a kind: project node; received "${canonicalSlug}".`);
  8955	  }
  8956	  if (projectDoc.mtime !== expected_mtime) {
  8957	    throw new VaultConflictError(canonicalSlug, expected_mtime, projectDoc.mtime);
  8958	  }
  8959	
  8960	  const validation = validateVaultTool({});
  8961	  if (validation.summary.errorFiles > 0) {
  8962	    throw new Error(
  8963	      `finalize_project_meaning blocked: validate_vault found ${validation.summary.errorFiles} file(s) with errors. Repair them before finalizing.`,
  8964	    );
  8965	  }
  8966	
  8967	  const artifact = COMPILED_ONTOLOGY_CACHE.get({ includeIndexes: true });
  8968	  const brief = attachVaultValidation(
  8969	    queryCompiledOntology(artifact, {
  8970	      operation: 'agent_brief',
  8971	      project: canonicalSlug,
  8972	    }),
  8973	    { operation: 'agent_brief', project: canonicalSlug },
  8974	  );
  8975	  const context = projectMeaningContext(artifact, canonicalSlug, brief.readiness?.status);
  8976	  if (!context.graphHash || context.inventoryResult.status !== 'ready') {
  8977	    throw new Error(
  8978	      `finalize_project_meaning blocked: current project witness inventory is unavailable (${context.inventoryResult.reason ?? 'unknown'}).`,
  8979	    );
  8980	  }
  8981	  const sourceReceipt = context.projectSource.receipt;
  8982	  if (!sourceReceipt) {
  8983	    throw new Error('finalize_project_meaning blocked: a valid project source receipt is required first.');
  8984	  }
  8985	
  8986	  const competency = parseProjectCompetencyMarkdown(context.projectDoc.body);
  8987	  const witnessAssessment = deriveMeaningAssessment({
  8988	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  8989	    graphHash: context.graphHash,
  8990	    structure: { status: brief.readiness?.status },
  8991	    source: meaningSourceFromProjectSource(context.projectSource),
  8992	    competency: {
  8993	      contract: MEANING_COMPETENCY_CONTRACT,
  8994	      receiptVersion: 1,
  8995	      evaluator: MEANING_COMPETENCY_EVALUATOR,
  8996	      graphHash: context.graphHash,
  8997	      inventory: context.inventoryResult.inventory,
  8998	      questions: competency.questions,
  8999	    },
  9000	  });
  9001	  const unresolvedAnswered = witnessAssessment.dimensions.competency.questions.find(
  9002	    (row) => row.status === 'answered' && row.witnessStatus !== 'resolved',
  9003	  );
  9004	  if (unresolvedAnswered) {
  9005	    throw new Error(
  9006	      `finalize_project_meaning blocked: competency "${unresolvedAnswered.id}" is marked answered but its current witnesses do not resolve.`,
  9007	    );
  9008	  }
  9009	
  9010	  const receipt = finalizeProjectMeaningReceipt({
  9011	    vaultRoot: VAULT_ROOT,
  9012	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  9013	    projectBody: context.projectDoc.body,
  9014	    graphHash: context.graphHash,
  9015	    sourceFingerprint: sourceReceipt.sourceFingerprint,
  9016	    measuredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  9017	  });
  9018	  const meaningAssessment = readProjectMeaningAssessment(context.assessmentInput);
  9019	  return {
  9020	    ok: true,
  9021	    changed: true,
  9022	    contract: 'projectMeaningReceipt:v1',
  9023	    projectSlug: canonicalSlug,
  9024	    bodyDigest: receipt.bodyDigest,
  9025	    graphHash: receipt.graphHash,
  9026	    sourceFingerprint: receipt.sourceFingerprint,
  9027	    measuredAt: receipt.measuredAt,
  9028	    meaningAssessment,
  9029	  };
  9030	}
  9031	
  9032	function attachVaultValidation(result, args = {}) {
  9033	  const validation = validateVaultTool({});
  9034	  const pathsChecked = validation.pathDrift?.checked !== false;
  9035	  const driftCount = validation.pathDrift?.drifts?.length ?? 0;
  9036	  const errorCount = validation.summary.errorFiles;
  9037	  const frontmatterWarnings = validation.summary.warningFiles;
  9038	  const warningCount = frontmatterWarnings + driftCount;
  9039	  // **The sentence states what this check looked at.** It used to merge two kinds
  9040	  // of warning into one number and say only "validator or source-path warning(s)".
 10335	    totalUpdated: result.totalUpdated,
 10336	  };
 10337	}
 10338	
 10339	function renameConcept({ oldSlug, newSlug, confirm = false, overwrite = false, expected_mtime }) {
 10340	  requireNonBlankString(oldSlug, 'oldSlug');
 10341	  requireNonBlankString(newSlug, 'newSlug');
 10342	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
 10343	  requireOptionalBoolean(overwrite, 'overwrite');
 10344	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
 10345	  if (oldSlug === newSlug) {
 10346	    throw new Error('oldSlug and newSlug are identical.');
 10347	  }
 10348	  /*
 10349	   * ⚠️ **Names differing only in case are stopped here** (review 2026-08-16 — the
 10350	   * document actually disappearing was reproduced).
 10351	   *
 10352	   * The check above is a string comparison, so it treats `Auth` and `auth` as
 10353	   * different. macOS and Windows filesystems treat them as the **same file**, so
 10354	   * writing the new name and deleting the old one deleted what had just been
 10355	   * written — and this tool returned `ok: true, moved: true`. Measured:
 10356	   *
 10357	   * ```
 10358	   * rename_concept{oldSlug:"Auth", newSlug:"auth", confirm:true, overwrite:true}
 10359	   *   → ok:true, moved:true, backlinkUpdates:{totalUpdated:1}
 10360	   *   → neither Auth.md nor auth.md left on disk; references left dangling
 10361	   * ```
 10362	   *
 10363	   * The write layer guards it too (`applyAllOrNothing`'s same-file detection), but
 10364	   * that alone yields a **half-finished rename**: references point at the new name
 10365	   * while the filename on disk does not change. Half-finished is not success — say
 10366	   * plainly that it cannot be done here, and name the path that works.
 10367	   */
 10368	  if (oldSlug.toLowerCase() === newSlug.toLowerCase()) {
 10369	    throw new Error(
 10370	      `oldSlug and newSlug differ only in letter case ("${oldSlug}" → "${newSlug}"). ` +
 10371	        'On macOS and Windows those are the same file, so this rename would delete the ' +
 10372	        'document instead of renaming it. Rename through a different name first ' +
 10373	        `(for example "${newSlug}-tmp"), then to "${newSlug}".`,
 10374	    );
 10375	  }
 10376	  if (!vaultSlugExists(VAULT_ROOT, oldSlug)) {
 10377	    throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Source slug does not exist in vault', oldSlug));
 10378	  }
 10379	  const targetExists = vaultSlugExists(VAULT_ROOT, newSlug);
 10380	  if (!overwrite && targetExists) {
 10381	    throw new Error(
 10382	      `Target slug already exists: "${newSlug}". Pass overwrite: true to replace it.`,
 10383	    );
 10384	  }
 10385	
 10386	  const sourcePath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, oldSlug);
 10387	  const targetPath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, newSlug);
 10388	  const sourceDoc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, sourcePath);
 10389	  const targetDoc = overwrite && targetExists ? readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, targetPath) : null;
 10390	
 10391	  // Slug flatness — rename writes directly rather than through writeDoc, so the
 10392	  // same gate is applied here (closing the door on path-shaped identity returning
 10393	  // through rename).
 10394	  const renameSlugIssue = flatSlugIssue(sourceDoc.frontmatter?.kind, newSlug);
 10395	  if (renameSlugIssue) throw new Error(renameSlugIssue);
 10396	
 10397	  // Source mtime conflict guard — compare against `expected` right after the read.
 10398	  if (typeof expected_mtime === 'number' && sourceDoc.mtime !== expected_mtime) {
 10399	    throw new VaultConflictError(oldSlug, expected_mtime, sourceDoc.mtime);
 10400	  }
 10401	
 10402	  // Step 1 — dry-run preview of every backlink rewrite.
 10403	  // An overwrite target is about to be replaced wholesale by the source document.
 10404	  // Planning backlink rewrites for that stale target inverts the order: right
 10405	  // after the source is written, the stale target overwrites it again.
 10406	  const replacedSlugs = overwrite ? [newSlug] : [];
 10407	  const preview = redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, oldSlug, newSlug, {
 10408	    dryRun: true,
 10409	    excludeSlugs: replacedSlugs,
 10410	  });
 10411	
 10412	  if (!confirm) {
 10413	    return {
 10414	      ok: false,
 10415	      dryRun: true,
 10416	      ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: true, wouldChange: true }),
 10417	      uid: sourceDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10418	      oldSlug,
 10419	      newSlug,
 10420	      sourcePath,
 10421	      targetPath,
 10422	      moved: false,
 10423	      backlinkUpdates: publicBacklinkUpdates(preview),
 10424	      message: `dry-run — confirm:true 를 주면 파일 이동 + ${preview.totalUpdated} 곳 backlink redirect 가 실제 적용됩니다.`,
 10425	    };
 10426	  }
 10427	
 10428	  /**
 10429	   * Step 2 — **three steps bound into one plan, applied all-or-nothing.**
 10430	   *
 10431	   * It used to write each step immediately in order: create the new file, rewrite
 10432	   * backlinks, delete the old file. The comment claimed *"partial failure doesn't
 10433	   * lose data"*, which was true (no data is lost) — but **the graph split**.
 10434	   * Measured 2026-08-01: with one of three references read-only, two nodes with
 10435	   * the same title remained and the references forked across both names. And
 10436	   * `validate` and `health` both called that vault clean. The tool description's
 10437	   * promise of "one atomic graph-level operation" was false.
 10438	   *
 10439	   * Now only the plan is built (`deferWrite`) and applied once at the end. On
 10440	   * failure it rolls back — as long as the process lives, the vault is as it started.
 10441	   */
 10442	  const nextFrontmatter = { ...sourceDoc.frontmatter };
 10443	  if (typeof nextFrontmatter.slug === 'string') {
 10444	    nextFrontmatter.slug = newSlug;
 10445	  }
 10446	  const result = redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, oldSlug, newSlug, {
 10447	    dryRun: false,
 10448	    deferWrite: true,
 10449	    excludeSlugs: replacedSlugs,
 10450	  });
 10451	  applyAllOrNothing([
 10452	    {
 10453	      op: 'write',
 10454	      path: targetPath,
 10455	      content: buildMarkdown({ frontmatter: nextFrontmatter, body: sourceDoc.body }),
 10456	      ...(targetDoc
 10457	        ? { expectedRaw: targetDoc.raw, expectedMtime: targetDoc.mtime }
 10458	        : { expectedAbsent: true }),
 10459	    },
 10460	    ...result.plan,
 10461	    // Deletion is last, and the plan preserves that order. Rollback runs in
 10462	    // reverse, so the old file is restored before the new one is removed.
 10463	    ...(sourcePath !== targetPath
 10464	      ? [{
 10465	          op: 'delete',
 10466	          path: sourcePath,
 10467	          expectedRaw: sourceDoc.raw,
 10468	          expectedMtime: sourceDoc.mtime,
 10469	        }]
 10470	      : []),
 10471	  ], { requireRevisions: true });
 10472	
 10473	  return {
 10474	    ok: true,
 10475	    dryRun: false,
 10476	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: false, wouldChange: false }),
 10477	    uid: sourceDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10478	    oldSlug,
 10479	    newSlug,
 10480	    sourcePath,
 10481	    targetPath,
 10482	    moved: true,
 10483	    backlinkUpdates: publicBacklinkUpdates(result),
 10484	    changed: true,
 10485	    postWriteMaintenance: compactPostWriteMaintenance(),
 10486	  };
 10487	}
 10488	
 10489	function looksLikeGeneratedStarter(body, kind) {
 10490	  const text = String(body || '');
 10491	  if (text.length > 800) return false;
 10492	  const markers = {
 10493	    project: /One- or two-line summary of this project/i,
 10494	    domain: /(?:Describe the stable responsibility or problem boundary|A \*domain\* is a large area of the project)/i,
 10495	    capability: /(?:Describe the observable, implementation-independent ability|A \*capability\* is one user-visible feature)/i,
 10496	    element: /(?:Describe the distinct implementation role|implementation element)/i,
 10497	    document: /(?:State what this narrative or reference artifact explains|source document)/i,
 10498	  };
 10499	  return Boolean(markers[kind]?.test(text));
 10500	}
 10501	
 10502	function reclassifyConcept({ slug, newKind, newSlug, domain, body, confirm = false, expected_mtime }) {
 10503	  requireNonBlankString(slug, 'slug');
 10504	  requireNonBlankString(newKind, 'newKind');
 10505	  if (!ADD_CONCEPT_KINDS.has(newKind)) throw new Error(formatAllowedValueError('newKind', newKind, [...ADD_CONCEPT_KINDS]));
 10506	  requireOptionalNonBlankString(newSlug, 'newSlug');
 10507	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
 10508	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
 10509	  if (domain !== undefined && domain !== null) requireNonBlankString(domain, 'domain');
 10510	  if (body !== undefined && typeof body !== 'string') throw new Error('body must be a string.');
 10511	  if ((newKind === 'capability' || newKind === 'element') && (domain === undefined || domain === null)) {
 10512	    throw new Error(`domain is required when reclassifying to kind "${newKind}".`);
 10513	  }
 10514	  const canonicalOld = resolveExistingVaultSlug(slug);
 10515	  if (!canonicalOld) throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Source slug does not exist in vault', slug));
 10516	  const canonicalNew = newSlug || canonicalOld;
 10517	  if (canonicalNew !== canonicalOld && vaultSlugExists(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalNew)) throw new Error(`Target slug already exists: "${canonicalNew}".`);
 10518	  const sourcePath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalOld);
 10519	  const targetPath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalNew);
 10520	  const sourceDoc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, sourcePath);
 10521	  if (typeof expected_mtime === 'number' && sourceDoc.mtime !== expected_mtime) throw new VaultConflictError(canonicalOld, expected_mtime, sourceDoc.mtime);
 10522	  const oldKind = sourceDoc.frontmatter.kind;
 10523	  // Slug flatness — reclassify writes directly too, so the new (kind, slug) pair is measured.
 10524	  const reclassifySlugIssue = flatSlugIssue(newKind, canonicalNew);
 10525	  if (reclassifySlugIssue) throw new Error(reclassifySlugIssue);
 10526	  const title = sourceDoc.frontmatter.title || canonicalNew.split('/').pop();
 10527	  let nextBody = sourceDoc.body;
 10528	  let bodyAction = 'preserved';
 10529	  if (body !== undefined) {
 10530	    nextBody = body;
 10531	    bodyAction = 'replaced_explicitly';
 10532	  } else if (looksLikeGeneratedStarter(sourceDoc.body, oldKind)) {
 10533	    nextBody = defaultBody(newKind, title);
 10534	    bodyAction = 'regenerated_starter';
 10535	  }
 10536	  const backlinkUpdates = canonicalNew === canonicalOld
 10537	    ? { updates: [], totalUpdated: 0 }
 10538	    : redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalOld, canonicalNew, { dryRun: true });
 10539	  const dryRun = !confirm;
 10540	  const base = {
 10541	    ok: false,
 10542	    dryRun,
 10543	    changed: false,
 10544	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun, wouldChange: true }),
 10545	    uid: sourceDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10546	    oldSlug: canonicalOld,
 10547	    newSlug: canonicalNew,
 10548	    oldKind,
 10549	    newKind,
 10550	    sourcePath,
 10551	    targetPath,
 10552	    bodyAction,
 10553	    backlinkUpdates: publicBacklinkUpdates(backlinkUpdates),
 10554	  };
 10555	  if (!confirm) return base;
 10556	  const nextFrontmatter = { ...sourceDoc.frontmatter, slug: canonicalNew, kind: newKind };
 10557	  if (domain === null || !['capability', 'element'].includes(newKind)) delete nextFrontmatter.domain;
 10558	  else if (domain !== undefined) nextFrontmatter.domain = domain;
 10559	  // One plan, for the same reason as rename: this tool also creates a file,
 10560	  // rewrites backlinks, and deletes the old file, and stopping midway left a
 10561	  // half-vault with a forked kind.
 10562	  const appliedBacklinks = canonicalNew === canonicalOld
 10563	    ? backlinkUpdates
 10564	    : redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, canonicalOld, canonicalNew, { dryRun: false, deferWrite: true });
 10565	  applyAllOrNothing([
 10566	    {
 10567	      op: 'write',
 10568	      path: targetPath,
 10569	      content: buildMarkdown({ frontmatter: nextFrontmatter, body: nextBody }),
 10570	      ...(sourcePath === targetPath
 10571	        ? { expectedRaw: sourceDoc.raw, expectedMtime: sourceDoc.mtime }
 10572	        : { expectedAbsent: true }),
 10573	    },
 10574	    ...(appliedBacklinks.plan ?? []),
 10575	    ...(sourcePath !== targetPath
 10576	      ? [{
 10577	          op: 'delete',
 10578	          path: sourcePath,
 10579	          expectedRaw: sourceDoc.raw,
 10580	          expectedMtime: sourceDoc.mtime,
 10581	        }]
 10582	      : []),
 10583	  ], { requireRevisions: true });
 10584	  return {
 10585	    ...base,
 10586	    ok: true,
 10587	    dryRun: false,
 10588	    changed: true,
 10589	    backlinkUpdates: publicBacklinkUpdates(appliedBacklinks),
 10590	    postWriteMaintenance: compactPostWriteMaintenance(),
 10591	  };
 10592	}
 10593	
 10594	function mergeConcepts({ fromSlug, intoSlug, confirm = false, expected_mtime, expected_into_mtime }) {
 10595	  requireNonBlankString(fromSlug, 'fromSlug');
 10596	  requireNonBlankString(intoSlug, 'intoSlug');
 10597	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
 10598	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
 10599	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_into_mtime, 'expected_into_mtime');
 10600	  if (fromSlug === intoSlug) {
 10601	    throw new Error('fromSlug and intoSlug are identical.');
 10602	  }
 10603	  if (!vaultSlugExists(VAULT_ROOT, fromSlug)) {
 10604	    throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('fromSlug does not exist in vault', fromSlug));
 10605	  }
 10606	  if (!vaultSlugExists(VAULT_ROOT, intoSlug)) {
 10607	    throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('intoSlug does not exist in vault', intoSlug));
 10608	  }
 10609	
 10610	  const fromPath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, fromSlug);
 10611	  const fromDoc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, fromPath);
 10612	  const intoPath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, intoSlug);
 10613	  const intoDoc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, intoPath);
 10614	  const identityHistory = mergeNodeIdentityHistory(fromDoc.frontmatter, intoDoc.frontmatter);
 10615	  const absorbedUids = identityHistory.absorbedUids;
 10616	
 10617	  // R11 closeout — fromSlug mtime conflict guard.
 10618	  if (typeof expected_mtime === 'number' && fromDoc.mtime !== expected_mtime) {
 10619	    throw new VaultConflictError(fromSlug, expected_mtime, fromDoc.mtime);
 10620	  }
 10621	  if (typeof expected_into_mtime === 'number' && intoDoc.mtime !== expected_into_mtime) {
 10622	    throw new VaultConflictError(intoSlug, expected_into_mtime, intoDoc.mtime);
 10623	  }
 10624	
 10625	  const preview = redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, fromSlug, intoSlug, { dryRun: true });
 10626	
 10627	  if (!confirm) {
 10628	    return {
 10629	      ok: false,
 10630	      dryRun: true,
 10631	      ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: true, wouldChange: true }),
 10632	      fromUid: fromDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10633	      intoUid: intoDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10634	      absorbedUids,
 10635	      fromSlug,
 10636	      intoSlug,
 10637	      fromPath,
 10638	      deleted: false,
 10639	      backlinkUpdates: publicBacklinkUpdates(preview),
 10640	      capturedFrom: {
 10641	        frontmatter: fromDoc.frontmatter,
 10642	        bodyExcerpt: extractSummaryExcerpt(fromDoc.body, 200),
 10643	      },
 10644	      message: `dry-run — confirm:true 를 주면 ${preview.totalUpdated} 곳 backlink redirect 후 ${fromSlug}.md 가 영구 삭제됩니다.`,
 10645	    };
 10646	  }
 10647	
 10648	  // Rewrite plus delete in one plan. Rewrites used to be written per file
 10649	  // immediately with the delete separate — if one file failed to write, only some
 10650	  // references pointed at the new name and `fromSlug` survived (and both checks
 10651	  // reported clean).
 10652	  const result = redirectBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, fromSlug, intoSlug, {
 10653	    dryRun: false,
 10654	    deferWrite: true,
 10655	  });
 10656	  const intoPlanIndex = result.plan.findIndex((operation) => operation.path === intoPath);
 10657	  const redirectedInto = intoPlanIndex >= 0
 10658	    ? parseFrontmatter(result.plan[intoPlanIndex].content)
 10659	    : { frontmatter: intoDoc.frontmatter, body: intoDoc.body };
 10660	  const intoIdentityWrite = {
 10661	    op: 'write',
 10662	    path: intoPath,
 10663	    expectedRaw: intoDoc.raw,
 10664	    expectedMtime: intoDoc.mtime,
 10665	    content: buildMarkdown({
 10666	      frontmatter: {
 10667	        ...redirectedInto.frontmatter,
 10668	        uid: identityHistory.survivorUid,
 10669	        merged_uids: identityHistory.merged_uids,
 10670	      },
 10671	      body: redirectedInto.body,
 10672	    }),
 10673	  };
 10674	  if (intoPlanIndex >= 0) result.plan[intoPlanIndex] = intoIdentityWrite;
 10675	  else result.plan.push(intoIdentityWrite);
 10676	  applyAllOrNothing([
 10677	    ...result.plan,
 10678	    {
 10679	      op: 'delete',
 10680	      path: fromPath,
 10681	      expectedRaw: fromDoc.raw,
 10682	      expectedMtime: fromDoc.mtime,
 10683	    },
 10684	  ], { requireRevisions: true });
 10685	
 10686	  return {
 10687	    ok: true,
 10688	    dryRun: false,
 10689	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: false, wouldChange: false }),
 10690	    fromUid: fromDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10710	// Slice 0 — absorb_document. Mirror of cli/src/commands/absorb.mjs's write
 10711	// path; core plan logic lives in ./absorb.mjs (mirrored at
 10712	// cli/src/lib/absorb.mjs, kept in lock-step by
 10713	// tests/contract/absorb.contract.test.ts).
 10714	function absorbDocumentTool({ filePath, confirm = false, allowOutsideRepo = false }) {
 10715	  requireNonBlankString(filePath, 'filePath');
 10716	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
 10717	  requireOptionalBoolean(allowOutsideRepo, 'allowOutsideRepo');
 10718	  const requestedPath = resolve(filePath);
 10719	  if (!existsSync(requestedPath) || !statSync(requestedPath).isFile()) {
 10720	    throw new Error(`file not found: ${requestedPath}`);
 10721	  }
 10722	  // Resolve symlinks before enforcing the boundary. Otherwise a path that
 10723	  // appears to live inside repoRoot could rewrite a target outside it.
 10724	  const abs = realpathSync(requestedPath);
 10725	  const canonicalRepoRoot = realpathSync(REPO_ROOT);
 10726	  const repoRelative = relative(canonicalRepoRoot, abs);
 10727	  const outsideRepo = repoRelative === '..' || repoRelative.startsWith(`..${sep}`);
 10728	  const backupPath = `${abs}${ABSORB_BACKUP_SUFFIX}`;
 10729	  const blockedReasons = [
 10730	    ...(outsideRepo && !allowOutsideRepo
 10731	      ? [
 10732	          `source file is outside repoRoot (${canonicalRepoRoot}); repeat with allowOutsideRepo:true only after reviewing the absolute path`,
 10733	        ]
 10734	      : []),
 10735	    ...(existsSync(backupPath)
 10736	      ? [`backup already exists and would be overwritten: ${backupPath}`]
 10737	      : []),
 10738	  ];
 10739	  const raw = readFileSync(abs, 'utf-8');
 10740	  const sourceLabel = basename(abs).replace(/\.md$/i, '');
 10741	  const plan = buildAbsorptionPlan(raw, {
 10742	    sourceLabel,
 10743	    isSlugTaken: (slug) => existsSync(slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, slug)),
 10744	  });
 10745	
 10746	  const sectionsOut = plan.sections.map((section) => ({
 10747	    heading: section.heading,
 10748	    category: section.category,
 10749	    kind: section.kind,
 10750	    role: section.role,
 10751	    confidence: section.confidence,
 10752	    action: section.action,
 10753	    targetSlug: section.targetSlug,
 10754	    injectionSuspect: section.injection.suspect,
 10755	    injectionMatches: section.injection.matches.map((m) => m.pattern),
 10756	  }));
 10757	
 10758	  if (!confirm) {
 10759	    return {
 10760	      ok: false,
 10761	      dryRun: true,
 10762	      ...destructivePreviewState({
 10763	        dryRun: true,
 10764	        wouldChange: true,
 10765	        blockedReasons,
 10766	      }),
 10767	      filePath: abs,
 10768	      outsideRepo,
 10769	      sourceLabel,
 10770	      title: plan.title,
 10771	      summary: plan.summary,
 10772	      sections: sectionsOut,
 10773	      message:
 10774	        `dry-run — ${plan.summary.absorbed} section(s) would be absorbed as document/policy nodes, ` +
 10775	        `${plan.summary.suggested} suggested (not written), ${plan.summary.injectionSuspect} injection-suspect ` +
 10776	        `(excluded from absorption). ` +
 10777	        (blockedReasons.length > 0
 10778	          ? `Confirmation is blocked: ${blockedReasons.join('; ')}.`
 10779	          : 'Pass confirm:true to write.'),
 10780	    };
 10781	  }
 10782	
 10783	  if (outsideRepo && !allowOutsideRepo) {
 10784	    throw new Error(
 10785	      `absorb_document blocked: source file is outside repoRoot (${canonicalRepoRoot}): ${abs}. ` +
 10786	        'Run a dry-run, review the absolute path, then pass allowOutsideRepo:true only if this rewrite is intended.',
 10787	    );
 10788	  }
 10789	  if (existsSync(backupPath)) {
 10790	    throw new Error(
 10791	      `backup already exists, refusing to overwrite: ${backupPath} — remove or rename it first.`,
 10792	    );
 10793	  }
 10794	
 10795	  const written = [];
 10796	  for (const section of plan.sections) {
 10797	    if (section.action !== 'absorb') continue;
 10798	    const fm = buildFrontmatter({
 10799	      slug: section.targetSlug,
 10800	      kind: 'document',
 10801	      title: section.targetTitle,
 10802	      role: 'policy',
 10803	      source: relative(VAULT_ROOT, abs),
 10804	      // Absorption is a write through this server too — same stamp, same identity source.
 10805	      [CREATED_BY_KEY]: agentProvenance(),
 10806	    });
 10807	    const body = `# ${section.targetTitle}\n\n${section.body}\n`;
 10808	    const writtenPath = writeDoc(VAULT_ROOT, section.targetSlug, { frontmatter: fm, body });
 10809	    written.push({ slug: section.targetSlug, filePath: writtenPath });
 10810	  }
 10811	
 10812	  // Backup *after* the vault writes succeed — if a write throws above, the
 10813	  // original source file is left untouched and the caller can retry safely.
 10814	  copyFileSync(abs, backupPath);
 10815	  const pointer = buildSlimPointer(plan);
 10816	  writeFileSync(abs, pointer, 'utf-8');
 10817	
 10818	  return {
 10819	    ok: true,
 10820	    dryRun: false,
 10821	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: false, wouldChange: false }),
 10822	    filePath: abs,
 10823	    outsideRepo,
 10824	    sourceLabel,
 10825	    title: plan.title,
 10826	    summary: plan.summary,
 10827	    sections: sectionsOut,
 10828	    written,
 10829	    backupPath,
 10830	    changed: true,
 10831	    message: `absorbed ${written.length} section(s) into the vault; source rewritten as a slim pointer (backup at ${backupPath}).`,
 10832	    postWriteMaintenance: compactPostWriteMaintenance(),
 10833	  };
 10834	}
 10835	
 10836	function deleteConcept({ slug, confirm = false, force = false, expected_mtime }) {
 10837	  requireNonBlankString(slug, 'slug');
 10838	  requireOptionalBoolean(confirm, 'confirm');
 10839	  requireOptionalBoolean(force, 'force');
 10840	  requireOptionalNonNegativeNumber(expected_mtime, 'expected_mtime');
 10841	  // Existence check, so a dry run never falsely reports "deletable". (deleteDoc
 10842	  // throws again at the real delete step, but the dry-run path never reaches
 10843	  // deleteDoc, hence the separate check.)
 10844	  const filePath = slugToPath(VAULT_ROOT, slug);
 10845	  if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
 10846	    throw new Error(missingSlugMessage('Doc not found', slug));
 10847	  }
 10848	  const sourceDoc = readDoc(VAULT_ROOT, filePath);
 10849	  const backlinks = findBacklinks(VAULT_ROOT, slug);
 10850	
 10851	  if (!confirm) {
 10852	    const blockedReasons =
 10853	      backlinks.length > 0 && !force
 10854	        ? [`${backlinks.length} backlink(s) require force:true before confirmation`]
 10855	        : [];
 10856	    return {
 10857	      ok: false,
 10858	      dryRun: true,
 10859	      ...destructivePreviewState({
 10860	        dryRun: true,
 10861	        wouldChange: true,
 10862	        blockedReasons,
 10863	      }),
 10864	      uid: sourceDoc.frontmatter.uid,
 10865	      slug,
 10866	      filePath,
 10867	      backlinks,
 10868	      message:
 10869	        backlinks.length > 0
 10870	          ? `dry-run — ${backlinks.length} 개 backlink 가 있어 confirm:true 만으로는 거부됩니다. force:true 까지 줘야 강행.`
 10871	          : 'dry-run — confirm:true 를 주면 실제 삭제됩니다.',
 10872	    };
 10873	  }
 10874	
 10875	  if (backlinks.length > 0 && !force) {
 10876	    throw new Error(
 10877	      `${backlinks.length} 개 backlink 가 있어 삭제 거부: ` +
 10878	        backlinks.map((b) => b.slug).join(', ') +
 10879	        ' — force:true 로 강행 가능 (참조 노드 dangling).',
 10880	    );
 10881	  }
 10882	
 10883	  const deleted = deleteDoc(VAULT_ROOT, slug, {
 10884	    expectedMtime: typeof expected_mtime === 'number' ? expected_mtime : undefined,
 10885	  });
 10886	  return {
 10887	    ok: true,
 10888	    dryRun: false,
 10889	    ...destructivePreviewState({ dryRun: false, wouldChange: false }),
 10890	    uid: deleted.frontmatter.uid,
 10891	    slug,
 10892	    filePath: deleted.filePath ?? filePath,
   170	  }
   171	  return opened;
   172	}
   173	
   174	function conflict(filename) {
   175	  return new Error(`Conflict: sidecar file changed before atomic write: ${filename}. Re-read and retry.`);
   176	}
   177	
   178	function unlinkOwnedTemporary(filePath, identity) {
   179	  if (!identity) return;
   180	  try {
   181	    const current = lstatOrNull(filePath);
   182	    if (current?.isFile() && !current.isSymbolicLink() && sameIdentity(current, identity)) {
   183	      unlinkSync(filePath);
   184	    }
   185	  } catch {
   186	    // A failed cleanup must never broaden into deleting a path we no longer own.
   187	  }
   188	}
   189	
   190	export function readVaultSidecarText(vaultRoot, filename) {
   191	  assertFilename(filename);
   192	  const context = sidecarContext(vaultRoot);
   193	  if (!context) return null;
   194	  const metadata = safeFileMetadata(context, filename);
   195	  if (!metadata) return null;
   196	  const filePath = sidecarFile(context, filename);
   197	  let descriptor = null;
   198	  try {
   199	    descriptor = openWithoutFollowing(filePath, constants.O_RDONLY);
   200	    assertOpenedFileMatches(metadata, descriptor, filename);
   201	    assertContext(context);
   202	    const text = readFileSync(descriptor, 'utf8');
   203	    const after = fstatSync(descriptor);
   204	    if (!sameRevision(revisionOf(metadata), revisionOf(after))) throw conflict(filename);
   205	    return { text, revision: revisionOf(after) };
   206	  } finally {
   207	    if (descriptor !== null) closeSync(descriptor);
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	export function createVaultSidecarTextExclusive(vaultRoot, filename, text) {
   212	  assertFilename(filename);
   213	  const context = sidecarContext(vaultRoot, { create: true });
   214	  const filePath = sidecarFile(context, filename);
   215	  const existing = safeFileMetadata(context, filename);
   216	  if (existing) return false;
   217	
   218	  let descriptor = null;
   219	  let createdIdentity = null;
   220	  let complete = false;
   221	  try {
   222	    try {
   223	      descriptor = openWithoutFollowing(
   224	        filePath,
   225	        constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL,
   226	        0o600,
   227	      );
   228	    } catch (error) {
   229	      if (error?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   230	        const raced = safeFileMetadata(context, filename);
   231	        if (raced) return false;
   232	      }
   233	      throw error;
   234	    }
   235	    createdIdentity = assertOpenedFileMatches(null, descriptor, filename);
   236	    assertContext(context);
   237	    writeFileSync(descriptor, text, 'utf8');
   238	    fsyncSync(descriptor);
   239	    complete = true;
   240	    return true;
   241	  } finally {
   242	    if (descriptor !== null) closeSync(descriptor);
   243	    if (!complete) unlinkOwnedTemporary(filePath, createdIdentity);
   244	  }
   245	}
   246	
   247	export function replaceVaultSidecarText(
   248	  vaultRoot,
   249	  filename,
   250	  text,
   251	  { expectedRevision = undefined, temporaryName = undefined } = {},
   252	) {
   253	  assertFilename(filename);
   254	  const context = sidecarContext(vaultRoot, { create: true });
   255	  const filePath = sidecarFile(context, filename);
   256	  const initial = safeFileMetadata(context, filename);
   257	  const initialRevision = revisionOf(initial);
   258	  if (expectedRevision !== undefined && !sameRevision(expectedRevision, initialRevision)) {
   259	    throw conflict(filename);
   260	  }
   261	
   262	  const tempFilename = temporaryName
   263	    ?? `.${filename}.oatlas-tmp-${process.pid}-${randomUUID()}`;
   264	  assertFilename(tempFilename);
   265	  if (tempFilename === filename) throw unsafe('Temporary sidecar filename must differ from target.');
   266	  const temporaryPath = sidecarFile(context, tempFilename);
   267	
   268	  let descriptor = null;
   269	  let temporaryIdentity = null;
   270	  let complete = false;
   271	  try {
   272	    descriptor = openWithoutFollowing(
   273	      temporaryPath,
   274	      constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL,
   275	      0o600,
   276	    );
   277	    temporaryIdentity = assertOpenedFileMatches(null, descriptor, tempFilename);
   278	    assertContext(context);
   279	    if (initial) fchmodSync(descriptor, initial.mode & 0o777);
   280	    writeFileSync(descriptor, text, 'utf8');
   281	    fsyncSync(descriptor);
   282	    closeSync(descriptor);
   283	    descriptor = null;
   284	
   285	    const currentRevision = revisionOf(safeFileMetadata(context, filename));
   286	    const requiredRevision = expectedRevision === undefined ? initialRevision : expectedRevision;
   287	    if (!sameRevision(requiredRevision, currentRevision)) throw conflict(filename);
   288	    assertContext(context);
   289	    renameSync(temporaryPath, filePath);
   290	    complete = true;
   291	    const committed = safeFileMetadata(context, filename);
   292	    return revisionOf(committed);
   293	  } finally {
   294	    if (descriptor !== null) closeSync(descriptor);
   295	    if (!complete) unlinkOwnedTemporary(temporaryPath, temporaryIdentity);
   296	  }
   297	}
   298	
   299	export function appendVaultSidecarLine(vaultRoot, filename, line) {
   300	  assertFilename(filename);
   300	  );
   301	}
   302	
   303	/**
   304	 * Whole-sidecar read. `malformed` is never treated as empty — overwriting an
   305	 * unreadable file would silently destroy another project's measurement.
   306	 */
   307	export function readProjectSourceBindings(vaultRoot) {
   308	  let stored;
   309	  try {
   310	    stored = readVaultSidecarText(vaultRoot, PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_FILENAME);
   311	  } catch (error) {
   312	    if (error instanceof SidecarPathError) {
   313	      return { status: 'unsafe_path', bindings: [], revision: null, error };
   314	    }
   315	    return { status: 'malformed', bindings: [], revision: null, error };
   316	  }
   317	  if (!stored) return { status: 'missing', bindings: [], revision: null };
   318	  let parsed;
   319	  try {
   320	    parsed = JSON.parse(stored.text);
   321	  } catch {
   322	    return { status: 'malformed', bindings: [], revision: stored.revision };
   323	  }
   324	  if (
   325	    !parsed
   326	    || parsed.contractVersion !== PROJECT_SOURCE_RECEIPT_VERSION
   327	    || !Array.isArray(parsed.bindings)
   328	    || !parsed.bindings.every(validBindingEnvelope)
   329	  ) return { status: 'malformed', bindings: [], revision: stored.revision };
   330	  return { status: 'ok', bindings: parsed.bindings, revision: stored.revision };
   331	}
   332	
   333	export function serializeProjectSourceState(bindings) {
   334	  return `${JSON.stringify({
   335	    contractVersion: PROJECT_SOURCE_RECEIPT_VERSION,
   336	    bindings,
   337	  }, null, 2)}\n`;
   338	}
   339	
   340	function commitState(vaultRoot, bindings, expectedRevision) {
   341	  createVaultSidecarTextExclusive(vaultRoot, '.gitignore', SIDECAR_IGNORE_CONTENT);
   342	  replaceVaultSidecarText(
   343	    vaultRoot,
   344	    PROJECT_SOURCE_STATE_FILENAME,
   345	    serializeProjectSourceState(bindings),
   346	    { expectedRevision },
   347	  );
   348	}
   349	
   350	/**
   351	 * Replace this project's binding, preserving every other project's. A malformed
   352	 * sidecar blocks the write instead of being clobbered — `repair` opts into
   353	 * discarding it, which is the only way out and must be a stated intent.
   354	 */
   355	export function writeProjectSourceBinding(vaultRoot, binding, { repair = false } = {}) {
   356	  const current = readProjectSourceBindings(vaultRoot);
   357	  if (current.status === 'unsafe_path') {
   358	    return { status: 'blocked_unsafe_path', bindings: [], replaced: 0, error: current.error };
   359	  }
   360	  if (current.status === 'malformed' && !repair) {
   361	    return { status: 'blocked_malformed', bindings: [], replaced: 0 };
   362	  }
   363	  const existing = current.status === 'ok' ? current.bindings : [];
   364	  const retained = existing.filter((candidate) => candidate.projectSlug !== binding.projectSlug);
   365	  const bindings = [...retained, binding];
   366	  try {
   367	    commitState(vaultRoot, bindings, current.revision);
   368	  } catch (error) {
   369	    if (error instanceof SidecarPathError) {
   370	      return { status: 'blocked_unsafe_path', bindings: existing, replaced: 0, error };
   371	    }
   372	    return { status: 'persistence_failed', bindings: existing, replaced: 0, error };
   373	  }
   374	  return { status: 'written', bindings, replaced: existing.length - retained.length };
   375	}
   376	
   377	/** Undo. Removes every binding for one project and leaves the rest untouched. */
   378	export function removeProjectSourceBindings(vaultRoot, projectSlug) {
   379	  const current = readProjectSourceBindings(vaultRoot);
   380	  if (current.status === 'unsafe_path') {
   381	    return { status: 'blocked_unsafe_path', bindings: [], removed: 0, error: current.error };
   382	  }
   383	  if (current.status === 'malformed') return { status: 'blocked_malformed', bindings: [], removed: 0 };
   384	  if (current.status === 'missing') return { status: 'not_bound', bindings: [], removed: 0 };
   385	  const retained = current.bindings.filter((candidate) => candidate.projectSlug !== projectSlug);
   386	  const removed = current.bindings.length - retained.length;
   387	  if (removed === 0) return { status: 'not_bound', bindings: current.bindings, removed: 0 };
   388	  try {
   389	    commitState(vaultRoot, retained, current.revision);
   390	  } catch (error) {
   391	    if (error instanceof SidecarPathError) {
   392	      return { status: 'blocked_unsafe_path', bindings: current.bindings, removed: 0, error };
   393	    }
   394	    return { status: 'persistence_failed', bindings: current.bindings, removed: 0, error };
   395	  }
   350	  vaultRoot,
   351	  projectSlug,
   352	  projectBody,
   353	  graphHash,
   354	  sourceFingerprint,
   355	  measuredAt,
   356	}) {
   357	  if (typeof vaultRoot !== 'string' || vaultRoot.length === 0) throw new Error('vaultRoot is required');
   358	  if (!safeProjectSlug(projectSlug)) throw new Error('projectSlug is invalid');
   359	  if (!safeGraphHash(graphHash)) throw new Error('graphHash is invalid');
   360	  if (!safeOpaque(sourceFingerprint)) throw new Error('sourceFingerprint is invalid');
   361	  if (!safeMeasuredAt(measuredAt)) throw new Error('measuredAt is invalid');
   362	  parseProjectCompetencyMarkdown(projectBody);
   363	
   364	  const existing = readState(vaultRoot);
   365	  if (existing.status === 'unsafe_path') throw existing.error;
   366	  if (existing.status === 'malformed') throw new Error('Existing project meaning receipt is malformed');
   367	  const receipt = {
   368	    version: PROJECT_MEANING_RECEIPT_VERSION,
   369	    evaluator: MEANING_COMPETENCY_EVALUATOR,
   370	    markdownContract: PROJECT_COMPETENCY_MARKDOWN_CONTRACT,
   371	    projectSlug,
   372	    bodyDigest: bodyDigest(projectBody),
   373	    graphHash,
   374	    sourceFingerprint,
   375	    measuredAt,
   376	  };
   377	  const retained = (existing.state?.receipts ?? []).filter((row) => row.projectSlug !== projectSlug);
   378	  const state = {
   379	    version: PROJECT_MEANING_RECEIPT_VERSION,
   380	    receipts: [...retained, receipt].sort((left, right) => left.projectSlug.localeCompare(right.projectSlug)),
   381	  };
   382	  replaceVaultSidecarText(
   383	    vaultRoot,
   384	    PROJECT_MEANING_STATE_FILENAME,
   385	    `${JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)}\n`,
   386	    { expectedRevision: existing.revision },
   387	  );
   388	  return receipt;
   389	}

   392	  return deriveMeaningAssessment({

codex
핵심은 Atlas MCP가 에디터 이벤트를 구독하지 않는다는 점입니다. `get_concept` 등이 읽은 시점의 `mtime`과 원문 스냅샷을 저장해 두었다가, 쓰기 직전에 디스크 상태와 비교하는 낙관적 동시성 방식입니다. [vault.mjs:46](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/vault.mjs:46)

### 변경 감지 방식

1. `get_concept` 응답의 `mtime`을 다음 쓰기의 `expected_mtime`으로 전달합니다.

2. 쓰기 직전에 현재 `mtime`과 비교합니다.

   - 파일이 수정됨
   - 파일이 삭제됨
   - `mtime`이 1ms 이상 달라짐

   이 경우 `VAULT_CONFLICT`가 발생하고 쓰지 않습니다.

3. 단일 파일 쓰기도 `expectedRaw`로 원문 바이트를 다시 비교합니다. 따라서 `mtime`이 우연히 같아도 내용이 바뀌었으면 충돌로 처리합니다.

4. 다중 파일 작업은 모든 대상 파일의 `mtime`과 원문을 먼저 검사합니다. 하나라도 바뀌면 아무 파일도 쓰지 않으며, 쓰기 도중 뒤늦게 충돌하면 앞선 변경을 롤백합니다. [vault.mjs:1875](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/vault.mjs:1875)

충돌 시에는 `errorCode: "vault_conflict"`가 반환됩니다. `force:true`는 `delete_concept`의 backlink 무시 옵션일 뿐, mtime 충돌을 우회하지 않습니다. 다시 읽고 최신 `mtime`으로 재시도해야 합니다. [vault.mjs:57](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/vault.mjs:57)

### 쓰기 도구와 인자

| 도구 | 인자 |
|---|---|
| `git_snapshot` | `confirm?`, `expectedHead?`, `message?` — 확인 시 `expectedHead` 필수 |
| `finalize_project_meaning` | `projectSlug`, `expected_mtime` 필수 |
| `connect_project_source` | `projectSlug` 필수; `rootPath?`, `confirm?`, `repair?` |
| `disconnect_project_source` | `projectSlug` 필수; `confirm?` |
| `add_concept` | `slug`, `kind`, `title` 필수; `domain?`, `capabilities?`, `elements?`, `path?`, `body?`, `labels?` |
| `add_concepts` | `concepts` 필수. 각 행은 `add_concept`와 동일하며 최대 50개 |
| `add_relation` | `from`, `to`, `type` 필수; `why?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `add_relations` | `relations` 필수. 각 행은 `from`, `to`, `type`, `why?`, `expected_mtime?`; 최대 50개 |
| `patch_concept` | `slug` 필수; `frontmatter?`, `body?`, `expected_mtime?` — 실제로는 둘 중 하나 이상 필요 |
| `remove_relation` | `from`, `to`, `type` 필수; `confirm?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `replace_relation` | `from`, `oldTo`, `oldType`, `newTo`, `newType` 필수; `why?`, `confirm?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `rename_concept` | `oldSlug`, `newSlug` 필수; `confirm?`, `overwrite?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `reclassify_concept` | `slug`, `newKind` 필수; `newSlug?`, `domain?`, `body?`, `confirm?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `merge_concepts` | `fromSlug`, `intoSlug` 필수; `confirm?`, `expected_mtime?`, `expected_into_mtime?` |
| `delete_concept` | `slug` 필수; `confirm?`, `force?`, `expected_mtime?` |
| `absorb_document` | `filePath` 필수; `confirm?`, `allowOutsideRepo?` |

`type`은 `depends_on`, `relates`, `contains`, `describes`, `domains`, `capabilities`, `elements`, `domain` 중 하나입니다. 새 `depends_on`에는 `why`가 필수입니다.

### 도구별 충돌 보호

- `add_concept`, `add_concepts`: 새 파일 생성 도구라 `expected_mtime`은 없습니다. 이미 파일이 있거나 외부 에디터가 먼저 같은 파일을 만들면 거부됩니다.
- `add_relation`, `add_relations`: source 노드의 `expected_mtime`을 검사합니다. 배치에서 같은 `from`을 여러 번 수정하면 첫 행 이후 mtime이 바뀌므로 같은 mtime을 재사용하면 실패합니다.
- `patch_concept`, `remove_relation`, `replace_relation`, `delete_concept`: 대상 노드의 `expected_mtime`을 검사합니다.
- `rename_concept`, `reclassify_concept`: 원본 노드의 mtime과 backlink 대상 파일들의 스냅샷을 검사합니다.
- `merge_concepts`: 삭제될 노드에는 `expected_mtime`, 살아남을 노드에는 `expected_into_mtime`을 각각 전달해야 양쪽 변경을 보호할 수 있습니다.
- `finalize_project_meaning`: 프로젝트 Markdown의 mtime을 검사한 뒤 별도의 receipt를 씁니다.
- `connect_project_source`, `disconnect_project_source`: 사용자 인자로 mtime을 받지는 않습니다. 대신 `.ontology-atlas/project-sources.json`을 전체 revision(`dev`, `ino`, `size`, `mtime`, `ctime`)으로 보호합니다. [vault-sidecar.mjs:247](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/vault-sidecar.mjs:247)
- `git_snapshot`: 파일 mtime이 아니라 Git `expectedHead`만 검사합니다.
- `absorb_document`: 현재 구현에는 외부 원본 파일의 `expected_mtime` 검사가 없습니다. `confirm:true` 호출 중 원본이 다시 편집되면 마지막 `writeFileSync`가 그 변경을 덮을 가능성이 있는 예외입니다. [index.js:10714](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/index.js:10714)

실무 흐름은 다음과 같습니다.

```text
get_concept({slug})
→ 응답의 mtime 확보
→ 사용자/에이전트 승인
→ patch_concept({slug, ..., expected_mtime: mtime})
→ vault_conflict이면 다시 get_concept 후 재검토
```

참고로 `OATLAS_WRITE_CONSENT`는 외부 편집 감지가 아니라 MCP 쓰기 전 사람의 승인 게이트입니다. 모든 비읽기 도구에 적용됩니다.
